Experimental results show that density-specific training improves performance in severe fog conditions. In particular, for the very heavy fog class, recall improves from 0.076 to 0.232, corresponding to an absolute gain of 15.6 percentage points. These findings suggest that deploying multiple specialized models, rather than a single general-purpose model, can improve perception robustness for autonomous vehicles under challenging visibility conditions. Future work will extend this strategy to additional sensing modalities, including LiDAR and radar, and evaluate generalization across diverse weather scenarios.

</details>


### [158] [D^2-4DGS: Dual-Depth Guided Sparse-Camera 4D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01588)
*Jijian Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出D²-4DGS，一种稀疏相机动态4D高斯泼溅框架，通过双源深度先验来弥补稀疏视角下的几何监督不足，在9个数据集-视角设置下均取得最优PSNR，平均比最佳竞争者提升1.33dB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态4D高斯泼溅方法依赖密集多视角视频提供几何约束，采集成本高且限制稀疏相机部署。减少输入视角虽降低采集成本，但削弱了几何监督，导致结构缺失和浮动高斯伪影。单目深度虽有密集覆盖但尺度模糊且局部有偏，多视几何深度虽有坐标一致性但覆盖不完整，单一来源无法同时满足密集覆盖和可靠几何的需求。

Method: 提出D²-4DGS框架：(1)将单目深度估计与有效的多视几何深度对齐，通过一致性验证识别可靠的几何锚点；(2)利用验证后的锚点进行一致性感知剪枝和深度监督；(3)结合验证的几何深度和对齐的仅单目深度估计，为欠重建区域提供候选几何用于致密化；(4)采用RGB-D联合优化在稀疏视角监督下提升外观保真度和几何一致性。

Result: 在全部9个数据集-视角组合设置中，D²-4DGS均取得最高PSNR，平均比每个设置中的最佳竞争方法提升1.33dB。

Conclusion: 通过利用单目深度与多视几何深度的互补性，D²-4DGS在稀疏相机设置下有效缓解了几何监督不足的问题，实现了更高的重建质量与渲染保真度，展示了双源深度先验在稀疏动态场景重建中的有效性。

Abstract: Dynamic 4D Gaussian Splatting has emerged as an efficient representation for dynamic novel view synthesis through explicit scene modeling and real-time rendering. However, existing methods typically require dense multi-view videos for sufficient geometric constraints, making capture expensive and limiting sparse-camera deployment. Reducing input views lowers acquisition cost but weakens geometry supervision, often causing missing structures and floating Gaussians. Depth priors provide geometric cues, yet no single source offers both dense coverage and reliable geometry. Monocular depth provides dense structure but is scale-ambiguous and locally biased, whereas multi-view geometric depth provides incomplete anchors consistent with the reconstruction coordinate system. To exploit their complementarity, we propose D$^2$-4DGS, a sparse-camera dynamic 4D Gaussian Splatting framework guided by dual-source depth priors. We align monocular estimates with valid multi-view geometric depths and verify their consistency to identify reliable geometric anchors. These verified anchors support consistency-aware pruning and depth supervision, while verified geometric depths and aligned mono-only estimates provide candidate geometry for densification in under-reconstructed regions. Finally, RGB-D joint optimization improves appearance fidelity and geometric consistency under sparse-view supervision. Across all nine dataset--view settings, D$^2$-4DGS achieves the highest PSNR, improving by 1.33 dB on average over the best competing method in each setting.

</details>


### [159] [When Measurement Conventions Masquerade as Calibration Gains in Cardiac Digital Twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01602)
*Dang P. M. Cao,Hieu Pham*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对心脏数字孪生中超声心动图EF（射血分数）观测算子的校准偏差问题进行系统审计，提出了"约定感知EF审计"（Convention-Aware EF Audit）协议。通过跨四个共享骨干的超声EF前端实验，发现相位条件化看似消除了CAMUS基线偏差，但匹配参考分析否定了这一收益：单平面真实EF误差在各模型间统计上无差异，而单平面真实EF比CAMUS双平面临床EF高出6.30分，这解释了几乎全部基线偏差。预设的EchoNet-Dynamic复现实验（对齐心尖四腔平面）消除了基线高估并逆转了CAMUS排名。研究还量化了血流动力学效应、保形残差宽度预算及EF分层变化，从而将真实的观测算子校准与测量伪影区分开来。代码已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心脏数字孪生通过观测算子将临床图像转换为生理测量值，但现有校准研究往往假定固定的参考约定（reference convention），未考虑超声EF测量中不同观察平面对齐和参考标准（如单平面vs双平面）带来的系统性偏差，导致校准结果可能混淆了真实算子优化和测量伪影。

Method: ①在四个共享骨干的超声EF前端上测试相位条件化（phase conditioning）的效果；②采用匹配参考（matched-reference）分析重新评估基线偏差，比较单平面真实EF误差和CAMUS双平面临床EF；③预设EchoNet-Dynamic复现实验，将提取器对齐到心尖四腔平面并公开数据；④量化血流动力学效应、保形残差宽度预算和EF分层变化，提出Convention-Aware EF Audit协议。

Result: 相位条件化看似消除了CAMUS基线偏差，但匹配参考分析显示各模型单平面真实EF误差在统计上不可区分；单平面真实EF比CAMUS双平面临床EF高6.30分，解释了几乎全部基线偏差。EchoNet-Dynamic复现消除了基线高估并逆转了CAMUS排名。

Conclusion: 现有EF观测算子校准偏差主要源于参考约定不一致（单平面与双平面EF之间的系统性差异）而非模型本身的能力差异；需要采用约定感知审计协议来区分真实校准与测量伪影。

Abstract: Cardiac digital twins convert clinical images into physiological measurements through observation operators, yet calibration studies often assume a fixed reference convention. Across four shared-backbone echocardiographic EF front-ends, phase conditioning appears to remove CAMUS baseline bias. Matched-reference analysis rejects this gain: singleplane ground-truth EF error is statistically indistinguishable across models, while single-plane ground-truth EF exceeds CAMUS biplane clinical EF by +6.30 points, explaining nearly all baseline bias. A prespecified EchoNet-Dynamic replication, with released data and our extractor aligned to the apical four-chamber plane, removes baseline overestimation and reverses the CAMUS ranking. We also quantify haemodynamic effects, conformal residual-width budgets, and EF-stratum changes, yielding a Convention-Aware EF Audit protocol that separates genuine observation operator calibration from measurement artefacts. GitHub: EjectionFraction-Bias-in-Cardiac-Digital-Twin.git

</details>


### [160] [Linear Multi-Timescale Retention as a Memory-Efficient Vision-Language Bridge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01614)
*Ashfak Yeafi,Mehedi Hasan,Md Khairul Islam*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LIA-MTR模块，一种内存高效的跨模态桥接方案，以O(N)复杂度替代视觉语言模型中O(N²)的Softmax注意力机制，通过ELU正特征映射、自适应写门控和log-线性分布递归衰减实现无限上下文视觉语言集成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型处理高分辨率图像时面临O(N²)内存复杂度的Softmax多头注意力的计算瓶颈；虽然独立MLP替代可达到O(N)缩放，但会丧失空间序列路由能力，导致全局场景理解和物体持久性严重退化。

Method: 提出LIA-MTR模块：(1) 使用ELU基正特征映射，(2) 自适应写门控机制，(3) log-线性分布的递归衰减，将连续视觉序列数学压缩为有界内存状态，实现严格O(N)序列交互复杂度。

Result: (1) 在16,000 token合成检索中无"中间丢失"退化；(2) 原生处理262,144个视觉块仅需11.2GB显存，标准MHA在16,384块时即OOM；(3) 665K指令微调后在MME基准上71.00% vs MLP基线68.11%，物体持久性提升10%。

Conclusion: 该工作为无限上下文视觉语言集成建立了数学严谨、计算平坦的基础，证明线性注意力机制可在保持全局语义理解与物体持久性的同时实现常数级内存扩展。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) face a critical computational bottleneck when processing high-resolution imagery due to the $O(N^2)$ memory complexity of Softmax Multi-Head Attention (MHA). While substituting MHA with independent Multi-Layer Perceptrons (MLPs) achieves $O(N)$ scaling, it strips the architecture of spatial sequence routing, severely degrading global scene understanding and object permanence. In this paper, we propose the Linear Multi-Timescale Retention (LIA-MTR) module, a memory-efficient cross-modal bridge. By integrating an ELU-based positive feature mapping with adaptive write-gating and log-linearly distributed recurrent decays, LIA-MTR mathematically compresses continuous visual sequences into bounded memory states. Theoretical analysis proves the architecture operates with strict $O(N)$ sequence-interaction complexity. Empirically, synthetic retrieval evaluations demonstrate that LIA-MTR flawlessly routes context across 16,000 tokens, eliminating the "Lost in the Middle" degradation typical of naive linear attention. Hardware benchmarking reveals infinite-context scaling capabilities, natively processing 262,144 visual patches within an 11.2 GB VRAM footprint, whereas standard MHA suffers out-of-memory failure at 16,384 patches. Furthermore, following instruction tuning on 665K conversational samples, LIA-MTR significantly outperforms an industry-standard MLP baseline on the MME benchmark (71.00% vs. 68.11%), driven by a 10% absolute improvement in object permanence and superior global semantic extraction. This work establishes a mathematically rigorous, computationally flat foundation for infinite-context Vision-Language integration.

</details>


### [161] [Motion Beyond Morphology: Bootstrapping Cross-Category Motion Transfer from Abstract Motion Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01628)
*Zhixue Fang,Zhimin Zhang,Bi'an Du,Zijie Meng,Yan Zhou,Wei Hu,Guoxin Zhang,Pengfei Wan,Kun Gai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出"超越形态的运动"视角，旨在实现跨形态差异（如不同类别、不同关节结构）的视频运动迁移，通过两阶段框架（多粒度抽象运动视图引导跨类别配对 + 直接参考视频条件生成）实现无需显式运动提取的端到端迁移，并发布OpenVMT数据集与基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频运动迁移方法依赖固定的结构对应关系，当参考视频与目标对象在形态、关节或形变机制上差异显著时，该对应关系不再成立，导致迁移效果退化。

Method: 提出两阶段框架：Stage I学习互补的多粒度抽象运动视图，利用这些视图引导跨类别视频对的构建，保留跨形态可迁移的动态；Stage II将该监督内部化为直接以参考视频为条件的生成模型，推理时无需显式提取运动。

Result: 在自建的OpenVMT-Dataset和OpenVMT-Bench上（覆盖Same、Near、Far三种类别差距），在运动保真度和目标物体保真度上均达到最先进水平。

Conclusion: 通过超越固定结构对应的运动抽象与直接条件生成，能够有效实现跨形态差异巨大的视频运动迁移，并提供了相应数据集与基准以推动该方向研究。

Abstract: Video motion transfer aims to animate a target object using dynamics from a reference video. Existing formulations largely rely on fixed structural correspondence, which becomes ill-defined when reference and target objects differ substantially in morphology, articulation, or deformation mechanisms. We introduce Motion Beyond Morphology, a perspective that seeks to transfer motion beyond fixed structural correspondence, by preserving dynamics that remain meaningful across different target morphologies. To realize this, we propose a two-stage framework. Stage~I learns complementary multi-granularity abstract motion views and uses them to bootstrap cross-category video pairs that preserve transferable dynamics across diverse morphologies. Stage~II internalizes this supervision into direct reference-video-conditioned generation, removing the need for explicit motion extraction at inference. We further introduce OpenVMT-Dataset and OpenVMT-Bench for training and evaluating image- and text-conditioned motion transfer across Same, Near, and Far category gaps, and plan to release both upon acceptance. Extensive experiments demonstrate state-of-the-art motion fidelity and target preservation. Project page: https://miniz233.github.io/MotionBeyondMorphology/

</details>


### [162] [Mitigating Visual Degradation in MLLMs via Spatial-Spectral Visual Anchor Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01635)
*Qianlong Yang,Bowen Ye,Xianda Guo,Yanlun Peng,Wenke Huang,Hongyuan Zhang,Yulei Jia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SSVAL框架，通过视觉锚点提示注入（VAPI）和空间/频域表示对齐损失，解决多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在视觉指令微调后内部表示发生漂移导致视觉感知退化的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型在视觉感知上仍存在缺陷。视觉指令微调后，MLLM内部表示在推理过程中迅速偏离其原始语义状态，导致严重的信息退化。现有方法尝试利用外部视觉基础模型（VFMs）对齐内部表示，但直接对齐虽能增强视觉语义，却无法缓解表示漂移问题。

Method: 提出SSVAL框架，核心是视觉锚点提示注入（VAPI）——在训练阶段注入能吸收外部VFM丰富知识的提示，使其在推理时充当稳定的视觉锚点来缓解表示漂移。同时引入辅助的空间域和频域表示对齐损失，在中间LLM层提供互补的视觉特定监督信号。

Result: 大量实验表明SSVAL显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过将外部VFM知识锚定到内部提示中并辅以空间-频域对齐监督，可以有效缓解MLLM推理时的表示漂移，提升视觉感知能力。

Abstract: Despite the progress of multimodal large language models (MLLMs), they continue to exhibit deficiencies in visual perception. Following visual instruction tuning, internal MLLM representations rapidly deviate from their original semantic states during inference, causing severe information degradation. While existing methods attempt to leverage external vision foundation models (VFMs) to align internal representations, we find that direct alignment with VFMs enhances visual semantics but fails to mitigate representation deviation. To address this, we propose Spatial-Spectral Visual Anchor Learning (SSVAL). The core of SSVAL is Visual Anchor Prompt Injection (VAPI), which introduces prompts that absorb rich knowledge from external VFMs during training, enabling them to serve as stable visual anchors that mitigate representation deviation during inference. Additionally, we incorporate auxiliary spatial and frequency-domain representation alignment losses to provide complementary vision-specific supervision at intermediate LLM layers. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SSVAL significantly outperforms existing methods. Code are available on our \href{https://msls38.github.io/SSVAL/}{project page}.

</details>


### [163] [Dynamic Resolution Routing for Efficient Egocentric Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01638)
*Huixin Sun,Wangbo Zhao,Fanyue Wei,Qiuxia Lin,Pengzhan Sun,Angela Yao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SmartRes，一种在像素空间通过动态分辨率路由实现效率优化的框架，用于第一人称（egocentric）视觉定位任务。它编码低分辨率全局视图，用轻量路由器激活对象中心区域的高分辨率块，并通过边缘正则化路由目标缓解前后景不平衡问题。在Ego4D和EgoIntention上减少了高达67%的视觉token，同时保留86.4%的全分辨率性能，推理速度比SOTA token缩减方法快1.66倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一人称视觉定位需要高分辨率输入来定位小物体，但多模态大语言模型在此领域扩展受限于视觉token处理的过高成本。现有基于token缩减的高效策略在选择对象中心的空间证据时不够可靠，因此需要在像素空间层面进行效率优化。

Method: SmartRes框架采用动态分辨率路由：(1) 先编码低分辨率视图获取全局上下文；(2) 使用轻量路由器激活对象中心区域的高分辨率块，并构建保序的视觉序列；(3) 引入边缘正则化路由目标（margin-regularized routing objective），增大前后景logit间隔，改善前景召回率，以应对严重的前后景不平衡问题。

Result: 在Ego4D和EgoIntention数据集上，SmartRes减少了高达67%的视觉token，保留86.4%的全分辨率性能；推理速度比SOTA token缩减方法快1.66倍且精度更高；在小物体定位方面表现优异。

Conclusion: SmartRes通过在像素空间而非token空间进行效率优化，为第一人称视觉定位提供了一种更可靠且高效的方法。其在小物体定位上的强性能表明该方法对第一人称应用场景具有实际有效性，代码将公开。

Abstract: Egocentric visual grounding requires high-resolution inputs to localize small objects. However, scaling Multimodal Large Language Models to this domain is constrained by the excessive cost of visual token processing. We identify that current efficient strategies based on token reduction are unreliable for selecting object-centric spatial evidence. To overcome this, we propose SmartRes, a framework that performs efficiency optimization in the pixel space via dynamic resolution routing. SmartRes first encodes a low-resolution view for global context and uses a lightweight router to activate high-resolution patches in object-centric regions and constructs an order-preserving visual sequence. To further enable robust routing under severe foreground-background imbalance, we introduce a margin-regularized routing objective that increases foreground-background logit separation and improves foreground recall. Experiments on Ego4D and EgoIntention show that SmartRes reduces visual tokens by up to 67% while retaining 86.4% of full-resolution performance, and achieves up to 1.66X faster inference than state-of-the-art token reduction methods with higher accuracy. Furthermore, strong performance on small object grounding indicates the effectiveness of SmartRes towards egocentric applications. Code will be publicly available.

</details>


### [164] [StreamTalk: Streaming Co-Speech Gesture Generation with Key-Pose Anchoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01643)
*Xiangyue Zhang,Jianfang Li,Jiaxu Zhang,Kaixing Yang,Steven Hoi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: StreamTalk提出了一种闭环流式共语音手势生成框架，通过周期性的"生成-检索-精炼"循环，利用尾部关键姿态作为目标锚点来限制长序列漂移，在BEAT2数据集上达到SOTA效果并以76 FPS实时运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流式共语音手势生成方法均为开环系统，每个片段仅依赖过去上下文，模型无法检查或修正其轨迹，导致小误差随序列增长而累积漂移。作者观察到这一失败主要源于缺乏前向约束而非短片段质量差。

Method: 1) 提出StreamTalk闭环框架，采用周期性的"生成-检索-精炼"循环：流式姿态引导生成先预测粗片段，从说话者特定动作数据库中检索合理尾部姿态，再以此姿态精炼片段后进入下一窗口。2) 训练时引入Stochastic Anchor Masking，随机遮蔽姿态和位移帧，让模型从稀疏边界条件恢复完整动作。3) 采用部分感知的DiT架构，分离手部、身体和位移流，减少全局位移与局部关节运动的干扰。

Result: StreamTalk在BEAT2数据集上取得了SOTA的FGD指标，相比开环基线显著降低长时漂移，并以76 FPS的速度实时运行。

Conclusion: 通过引入前向约束（尾部关键姿态锚点）将开环流式生成转变为闭环系统，有效解决了长序列漂移问题，验证了"漂移主要源于缺乏前向约束"这一核心观察的正确性。

Abstract: Real-time co-speech gesture generation must produce 3D motion clip by clip as speech arrives. Existing streaming methods are open-loop: each clip depends on past context, but the model cannot check or correct its trajectory. Small errors therefore accumulate and cause drift over long sequences. We observe that this failure is mainly caused by the lack of a forward constraint rather than poor short-clip quality. A plausible key pose at the end of each clip provides a destination anchor that limits drift. Based on this observation, we propose StreamTalk, a closed-loop framework with a periodic generate-retrieve-refine cycle. Streaming Pose-Guided Generation first predicts a coarse clip, retrieves a plausible tail pose from a speaker-specific motion database, and refines the clip using this pose before continuing to the next window. During training, Stochastic Anchor Masking randomly masks pose and translation frames, teaching the model to recover complete motion from sparse boundary conditions. A part-aware DiT separates hand, body, and translation streams to reduce interference between global displacement and local articulation. On BEAT2, StreamTalk achieves state-of-the-art FGD, reduces long-horizon drift relative to open-loop baselines, and runs in real time at 76 FPS. Project page: https://xiangyue-zhang.github.io/StreamTalk/.

</details>


### [165] [CRAFT: Compression via Recursive Adaptive Fusion of Video Tokens for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01644)
*Yu Chen,Xiaohong Li,Xiaole Wang,Jianjin Zhang,Jun Sun,Yafeng Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了CRAFT，一种通过递归自适应融合视频token的压缩方法，在不进行昂贵对齐训练的情况下实现高效压缩，8倍压缩时保留约97%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频理解中视觉token数量庞大导致预填充阶段计算和内存成本激增，现有token压缩方法要么采用启发式、无训练的压缩（缺乏内容自适应性），要么引入需要昂贵对齐训练的新模块，无法兼顾效率与自适应性。

Method: CRAFT通过递归合并token，将无参数token选择与可学习token融合解耦：全局相似度决定哪些token被合并，位置感知加权模块和内容自适应通道门控学习如何融合。整个压缩流水线是查询无关的，每个保留的token是原始token的线性组合，从而保持真实时空坐标并与预训练语言模型的输入分布对齐。

Result: 在多个代表性视频基准上，CRAFT一致优于先前的SOTA token压缩方法。在约8倍压缩率下，保留了主干网络约97%的平均准确率，并展现出显著的效率提升。

Conclusion: 通过递归自适应融合策略，CRAFT有效解决了视频token压缩中效率与内容自适应性之间的权衡问题，在不引入额外对齐训练成本的前提下实现了高质量的token压缩。

Abstract: In video understanding, vision-language models (VLMs) must ingest massive numbers of visual tokens, causing the computational and memory cost of the prefill stage to rise sharply. Such visual sequences are highly redundant along the spatio-temporal dimension, yet a high compression ratio is often accompanied by the loss of critical details. Existing token-compression methods either employ heuristic, training-free compression with limited content adaptivity or introduce additional modules that require expensive alignment training, leaving the trade-off between efficiency and adaptivity unresolved. To alleviate this limitation, we propose CRAFT: Compression via Recursive Adaptive Fusion of Video Tokens. CRAFT recursively merges tokens by decoupling parameter-free token selection from learnable token fusion: global similarity determines which tokens to merge, while a position-aware weighting module and a content-adaptive channel-wise gate learn how to fuse them. The whole compression pipeline is query-agnostic. Because every retained token is a linear combination of the original tokens, CRAFT preserves their true spatio-temporal coordinates and stays aligned with the pre-trained language model's input distribution. Experiments on multiple representative video benchmarks show that CRAFT consistently outperforms prior state-of-the-art token-compression methods. At about $8\times$ compression, it retains roughly $97\%$ of the backbone's average accuracy and shows significant efficiency improvement.

</details>


### [166] [StreamSplat: Streaming Feed-Forward 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01659)
*Changhao Song,Yuxuan Wang,Qibiao Li,Youcheng Cai,Ligang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: StreamSplat 提出了一种流式前馈 3D Gaussian Splatting 框架，通过 Voxel-Aligned Causal Cache、History-Projected Depth Anchoring 和 Cache-Guided Feature Injection 三个核心组件，在不依赖未来视角或全场景上下文的情况下，增量维护持久化的几何场景状态，实现长序列输入下的高效新颖视角合成，并在 DL3DV、RealEstate10K 和 ScanNet 数据集上验证了其可扩展性和竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈 3DGS 方法通常假设固定数量的上下文视角并联合处理，无法适应视角按序到达、场景需因果更新的在线场景。这限制了它们在长输入流、实时或在线应用中的适用性，因为固定视角基线在大量视角输入时会耗尽内存。

Method: StreamSplat 是一个流式前馈 3DGS 框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) Voxel-Aligned Causal Cache (VACC)——将历史 3D token 存储在内存有界的体素结构中，使内存随探索的几何范围而非序列长度增长；(2) History-Projected Depth Anchoring (HPDA)——将缓存的几何投影为深度引导，用于当前代价体（cost-volume）估计；(3) Cache-Guided Feature Injection (CGFI)——将缓存的潜在特征证据注入高斯 token 回归过程。框架按输入块增量更新持久场景状态，并解码为可渲染的 3D 高斯。

Result: 在 DL3DV、RealEstate10K 和 ScanNet 上，StreamSplat 在稀疏因果输入下与最先进的前馈 3DGS 方法保持竞争力；在 256、512 和 1024 视角的长输入流中，固定视角基线内存耗尽，而 StreamSplat 可正常运行，且随着更多观测到达，新颖视角合成质量持续提升。

Conclusion: StreamSplat 证明了流式前馈 3DGS 的可行性：通过内存有界的缓存机制和因果预测策略，在不使用未来视角或全场景上下文的限制下，不仅能够匹敌现有方法在稀疏输入下的性能，还能扩展到远超固定视角基线能力范围的长序列输入，展示了在线 3D 重建场景中的实用潜力。

Abstract: Feed-forward 3D Gaussian Splatting enables efficient novel-view synthesis without per-scene optimization, but most existing methods assume a fixed set of context views and process them jointly. This limits their applicability to online scenarios where calibrated views arrive sequentially and the scene must be updated causally. We present \emph{StreamSplat}, a streaming feed-forward 3DGS framework that incrementally maintains a persistent geometry-grounded scene state and decodes it into renderable 3D Gaussians after each input chunk. StreamSplat centers on a \textbf{Voxel-Aligned Causal Cache (VACC)}, which stores historical 3D tokens in a memory-bounded voxel structure so that memory grows with explored scene geometry rather than stream length. To better reuse history during causal prediction, we introduce \textbf{History-Projected Depth Anchoring (HPDA)} to project cached geometry as depth guidance for current cost-volume estimation, and \textbf{Cache-Guided Feature Injection (CGFI)} to inject cached latent evidence into Gaussian-token regression. Experiments on DL3DV, RealEstate10K, and ScanNet show that StreamSplat remains competitive with state-of-the-art feed-forward 3DGS methods under sparse causal inputs, despite not using future views or full-scene context. More importantly, it scales to long input streams with 256, 512, and 1024 views where fixed-view baselines run out of memory, yielding sustained improvements in novel-view synthesis quality as more observations arrive. The code will be made publicly available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [167] [Ground, Cover, and Refine: Evidence-Centric Frame Selection for Long-Video Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01660)
*Fan Wei,Siru Zhong,Runmin Dong,Miao Yang,Zhaoyang Luo,Haohuan Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GCR（Ground-Cover-Refine）框架，将固定帧预算下的长视频问答帧选择建模为联合证据策展问题，通过时间事件锚定、全局最大边际相关性和证据价值驱动的帧替换三个步骤，在无需训练的情况下显著提升长视频QA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频问答需要在有限视觉token预算内从数千帧中识别稀疏但关键的证据。现有方法存在两大局限：一是单遍选择导致选中帧聚集在局部相关峰值附近，预算耗尽后遗漏的证据无法恢复；二是文本与视觉证据之间对齐较弱，协同效果不佳。

Method: GCR是一个training-free框架，包含三个模块：(1) Ground：将带时间戳的文本转换为时间事件，选择查询相关的真实帧锚点，并将各事件文本渲染到其时序对齐的帧上；(2) Cover：用直接视觉锚点补充基于grounded events的帧，并通过全局最大边际相关（MMR）保持上下文多样性；(3) Refine：重新审视被遗漏的时间区域，仅当真实帧medoid具有更高证据价值时，用它替换最弱的可替换上下文帧。GCR保持固定数量的时序有序帧，无需VLM训练或架构修改。

Result: 在LongVideoBench和Video-MME两个基准上，跨3个7B骨干模型和8/32/64三种帧预算均取得一致改进。使用7B LLaVA-OV骨干和32帧时，两个基准分别达到64.25%和62.15%，比最强复现基线高出2.54和1.93个百分点。

Conclusion: GCR通过联合证据策展的思路有效解决了长视频QA中帧选择的两大痛点（局部聚类导致证据遗漏、文本-视觉弱对齐），在无需训练的前提下取得了显著的性能提升，为长视频问答提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Long-video question answering requires identifying sparse yet critical evidence from videos containing thousands of frames under a constrained visual-token budget. Existing methods either select query-aware frames in a single pass or rely on timestamped text solely as retrieval guidance, leading to two key limitations. First, selected frames tend to cluster around local relevance peaks, and once the budget is exhausted, omitted evidence cannot be recovered. Second, textual and visual evidence remain weakly aligned. We propose GCR, a training-free framework that casts fixed-budget frame selection as a joint evidence curation problem. Ground converts timestamped text into temporal events, selects query-relevant real frame anchors, and renders each event text onto its temporally aligned frame. Cover supplements grounded events with direct visual anchors for complementary visual evidence and applies global maximal marginal relevance to preserve diverse context. Refine revisits omitted temporal regions and replaces the weakest revisable context frame with a real-frame medoid---but only when the medoid offers greater evidence value. GCR maintains a fixed number of chronologically ordered frames and requires no VLM training or architectural modification. Experiments on LongVideoBench and Video-MME, across three 7B backbones and frame budgets of 8, 32, and 64, demonstrate consistent improvements in long-video QA. With the 7B LLaVA-OV backbone and 32 frames, GCR achieves 64.25% and 62.15% on the two benchmarks, outperforming the strongest reproduced baselines by 2.54 and 1.93 percentage points, respectively.

</details>


### [168] [FairForensics: Seeing Expressions and Parsing Demographics via Vision-Language Modeling for Generalizable Fair Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01661)
*Yaning Zhang,Jiao Wu,Zan Gao,Linlin Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FairForensics，一个基于视觉语言模型的公平深度伪造检测框架。该框架构建了人口统计学平衡的FDD基准，并通过表情感知视觉编码器与人口感知语言编码器的视觉-语言对齐，同时提升检测器在未见伪造手法上的泛化能力和跨人口统计群体的公平性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公平性增强的深度伪造检测器在面向未见伪造手法和不同人口统计群体时泛化能力不足，且通常在人口统计学不平衡的数据分布下训练和评估，导致对少数群体的预测产生偏差。

Method: 1) 构建人口统计学平衡的FDD基准，覆盖平衡和不平衡人群场景；2) 提出FairForensics框架，包含两个核心组件：(a) 面部伪造泛化增强：基于原始与伪造表情向量显著分布差异的观察，设计表情编码器捕获高层表情引导的伪造模式，并结合身份感知补丁扰动模块的表情感知视觉编码器，集成全局外观与表情伪造特征并减轻身份偏差；(b) 人口感知公平性正则化：设计人口引导语言编码器提取人群感知全局语言嵌入，通过视觉-语言对齐促进伪造特征与人口信息的解耦，并提出人口感知原型公平目标以增强跨人口子组的类间可分性和类内对齐。

Result: 在构建的平衡人口统计学基准上进行了大量实验，结果表明所提方法在泛化能力和公平性方面均达到最先进水平。

Conclusion: 通过人口平衡基准与视觉-语言对齐的联合设计，FairForensics有效解决了公平深度伪造检测中泛化性与公平性之间的权衡问题，为后续研究提供了新的基准和方法框架。

Abstract: The challenge of fair deepfake detection (FDD) has attracted increasing attention. Existing fairness-enhanced detectors often suffer from suboptimal generalization to unseen manipulations and fairness across demographic groups. They are typically developed and evaluated on demographically imbalanced distributions, resulting in biased predictions toward minority groups. In this paper, we construct a novel demographically balanced FDD benchmark to train and evaluate the fairness of detectors under both balanced and imbalanced population scenarios. Additionally, we introduce a novel expression and demographic perceptual vision-language model, termed FairForensics, for generalizable fair deepfake detection. FairForensics conducts face forgery generalization enhancement and demographic-aware fairness regularization. During face forgery generalization enhancement, built upon the novel observation of significant distribution differences between pristine and forged expression vectors, we design an expression encoder to capture high-level expression-guided forgery patterns, and an expression-perceptual visual encoder that integrates global appearance and expression forgery features while mitigating identity bias using an identity-aware patch perturbation module. Under demographic-aware fairness regularization, we propose a demographic-guided language encoder to extract population-aware global language embeddings, which boosts the decoupling of forgery features from demographic information via vision-language alignment. We devise a population-aware prototype fairness objective to enforce both inter-class separability and intra-class alignment across demographic subgroups. Extensive experiments on our balanced demographic benchmark show that our method achieves the state-of-the-art in terms of generalization and fairness.

</details>


### [169] [Few-Shot Concept Prompt Learning for Segmentation Foundation Models via Visual Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01663)
*Rahul Venkataramani,Rachana Sathish*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FS-CPL（Few-Shot Concept Prompt Learning），用小量图像-掩码对学习连续的概念提示嵌入，替代自然语言提示来控制可提示分割基础模型（如SAM3、Medical SAM3）。在四个医学影像基准上实现高达+0.62的Dice绝对提升，且与骨干网络无关，证明视觉概念提示与领域预训练互补。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可提示分割基础模型（SAM3、Medical SAM3）声称能通过自然语言接口实现少样本医学分割，但在临床任务上表现不佳。作者认为这不是医学预训练不足或提示措辞不佳所致，而是结构性的局限——在图像-文本配对监督稀缺的临床领域中该问题将长期存在，且局限性与自然语言作为控制信号本身有关。

Method: 提出FS-CPL：从K个图像-掩码对的小支撑集中，通过掩码监督学习一个连续的概念提示嵌入p*（维度T×d），编码器-解码器骨干保持冻结。该方法不依赖图像-文本配对数据，也不需要重新训练骨干网络。

Result: 在超声和内镜四个公开基准（BUSI、HC18、TN3K、CVC-Clinic）上，FS-CPL相比典型文本提示实现了高达+0.62的绝对Dice提升，且对骨干网络无关——同时对vanilla SAM3和领域专门预训练的Medical SAM3均有提升。

Conclusion: 自然语言提示在图像-文本监督稀缺的医学领域存在结构性性能瓶颈，而直接在目标分布上学习的视觉概念提示可以恢复丧失的性能，无需额外的图像-文本数据或骨干重训练。视觉概念提示与领域内预训练是互补而非替代关系。

Abstract: Promptable segmentation foundation models (FMs) such as SAM3 and Medical SAM3 promise few-shot, interactively-specified segmentation for medical imaging through a natural language interface, yet their performance on clinical tasks falls well short of this promise. We posit that this shortfall is not an artefact of insufficient medical pretraining or imperfect prompt phrasing, but a structural limitation that will persist in any domain where paired image-text supervision is scarce, as it is across most clinical modalities. We further hypothesize that the limitation is specific to natural language as a control signal: a visually grounded prompt, learned directly from the target distribution, should recover the lost performance without additional image-text data or backbone retraining. We propose Few-Shot Concept Prompt Learning (FS-CPL), which learns a continuous concept prompt embedding $\mathbf{p}^* \in \mathbb{R}^{T \times d}$ from a small support set of $K$ image--mask pairs via mask supervision, with the encoder-decoder backbone frozen. Across four public benchmarks spanning ultrasound and endoscopy (BUSI, HC18, TN3K, CVC-Clinic), FS-CPL delivers absolute Dice improvements of up to $+0.62$ over canonical text prompts and is \emph{backbone-agnostic}: it lifts both vanilla SAM3 and the domain-specifically pretrained Medical SAM3, showing that visual concept prompting is complementary to in-domain pretraining.

</details>


### [170] [FAU at ImageCLEF 2026 Task on Multimodal Reasoning Robust Candidate Scoring and Concise Multilingual Visual Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01664)
*Mohamed Basem,Vincent Christlein*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了ImageCLEF 2026多模态推理系统的设计与实现，系统针对视觉多项选择问答（Visual MCQ）和视觉开放问答（Visual OpenQA）两个子任务。核心观点是：可靠的输出控制与模型选择同等重要。对于MCQ任务，通过候选标签logits直接评分替代自由文本生成，并结合分数融合与投票；对于OpenQA任务，采用图像增强、简洁答案提示、确定性解码和定向后处理来消除推理痕迹与格式伪影。在无需任务特定训练的情况下，该系统在Visual MCQ中位列第三（0.7108准确率），在Visual OpenQA中位列第一（COMET 0.6488、BLEU 0.1391、ROUGE-L 0.2762、METEOR 0.2383），证明了推理工程（评分、集成、提示与清理）对提升强视觉语言模型可靠性的实践价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态问答任务面临两个核心挑战：一是包含密集文本、图表、公式和单位的复杂教育/科学图像需要可靠的多语言推理；二是需要严格遵守答案格式。现有视觉语言模型（VLM）在自由文本生成时容易产生推理痕迹和格式伪影，导致答案不可靠。作者假设"输出控制"这一推理工程层面被忽视的因素，其重要性不亚于模型选择本身，旨在探索如何在不进行任务特定训练的前提下，通过推理阶段的工程技术将强VLM转化为可靠的竞赛系统。

Method: 针对Visual MCQ子任务：放弃脆弱的自由文本生成，改为直接从VLM的logits中对候选标签进行打分，然后将多个互补运行的结果通过分数融合与投票方式组合。针对Visual OpenQA子任务：采用图像增强、简洁最终答案提示、确定性解码（去除了随机性），以及针对性的后处理（去除推理痕迹和格式化伪影）。整个系统不依赖任务特定的模型训练，纯粹依赖推理层面的工程手段。

Result: 在官方提交中：Visual MCQ子任务获得第三名，准确率达0.7108；Visual OpenQA子任务获得第一名，各项指标为COMET 0.6488、BLEU 0.1391、ROUGE-L 0.2762、METEOR 0.2383。研究结果表明，无需训练，仅通过推理工程即可取得优秀的竞赛成绩。

Conclusion: 推理工程具有显著的实践价值：精心设计的评分策略、模型集成、提示词工程和输出清理可以将强大的视觉语言模型转化为可靠的竞赛级系统。这一发现提示，在多模态问答系统中，输出结构的稳健控制可能比模型本身的提升更具性价比。

Abstract: We present our ImageCLEF 2026 Multimodal Reasoning system for the Visual Multiple Choice Question Answering (Visual MCQ) and Visual Open Question Answering (Visual OpenQA) subtasks. The challenge requires reliable reasoning over multilingual educational and scientific images with dense text, diagrams, charts, tables, formulas, and units, while enforcing strict answer formats. Our central finding is that robust output control is as important as model choice. For Visual MCQ, we replace fragile free-form generation with direct candidate label scoring from vision-language model logits, then combine complementary runs through score fusion and voting. For Visual OpenQA, we use image enhancement, concise final answer prompting, deterministic decoding, and targeted post-processing to remove reasoning traces and formatting artifacts. Without task-specific model training, our official submissions achieved third place in Visual MCQ with 0.7108 accuracy and first place in Visual OpenQA with 0.6488 COMET, 0.1391 BLEU, 0.2762 ROUGE L, and 0.2383 METEOR. The results highlight the practical value of inference engineering: careful scoring, ensembling, prompting, and cleanup can turn strong VLMs into reliable competition systems.

</details>


### [171] [Generative Brownian Bridge Diffusion In Motion Space For Enhanced Myocardial Strain Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01677)
*Rishov Paul,Frederick H. Epstein,Miaomiao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种基于布朗桥扩散模型的生成式框架，从常规采集的心脏磁共振（CMR）序列中合成运动衍生的心肌应变值，无需专门的应变成像采集即可获得高精度的心肌应变分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心脏磁共振图像的心肌应变分析对评估心脏功能至关重要，但现有技术要么依赖人工后处理且区域精度欠佳，要么需要专门且昂贵的成像采集，限制了临床广泛应用。

Method: 开发了一种运动空间中的布朗桥扩散模型，学习标准CMR运动（通过广泛采用配准方法估计）与先进应变成像技术提供的高精度运动之间的概率映射。模型以对应CMR图像为条件以促进生成过程中解剖结构的保真度。

Result: 在大规模多中心CMR数据集（包含配对的标准电影CMR和先进应变成像采集）上验证，实验表明该框架相比现有基于学习的方法显著提高了标准CMR的运动预测和应变分析准确性。

Conclusion: 该研究为开发经济有效、临床可部署的心脏功能评估AI工具提供了新范式，能够在繁忙的临床工作流中以增强的应变精度实现评估，代码已公开。

Abstract: Myocardial strain analysis of cardiac magnetic resonance (CMR) images provides an important tool for evaluating cardiac function. However, current techniques require either human-adjusted post-processing with suboptimal regional accuracy, or specialized and costly imaging acquisitions. In this paper, we propose to leverage the power of generative models to synthesize high-quality motion-derived strain values from routinely acquired CMR sequences. Specifically, we develop a novel Brownian bridge diffusion model in motion space to learn the probabilistic mapping between standard CMR motion estimated from widely adopted registration methods and highly accurate motion provided by advanced strain imaging techniques. To promote the fidelity of anatomical structure in the generation process, our model is conditioned on the corresponding CMR images. We validate our method on large-scale multi-center CMR datasets including subjects of paired standard cine CMR and advanced strain imaging acquisitions. Experimental results demonstrate that our framework significantly improves the accuracy of motion prediction and strain analysis from standard CMRs compared to existing learning-based approaches. Our research represents a new paradigm for potentially developing cost-effective, clinically deployable AI tools for cardiac function assessment with enhanced strain accuracy in busy clinical workflows. Our code is publicly available at anonymous.4open.science/r/Brownian-Bridge-strain-analysis-1140.

</details>


### [172] [Generative AI and Foundation Models in Medical Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01686)
*Masahiro Oda*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文概述了生成式AI（扩散模型、大语言模型）及基础模型在医学影像处理与医疗支持中的应用，并探讨了如何利用国家数据和计算资源开发面向医疗支持的基础模型与高性能AI。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI和基础模型的快速发展正在深刻改变AI开发范式，尤其在医学影像处理领域。作者希望通过系统梳理主流生成式AI技术（扩散模型、LLM）及其在医疗领域的应用，并讨论基础模型的构建方法，为利用国家层面数据与算力资源开发高性能医疗AI提供方向性指导。

Method: 本文为综述性论文，主要对图像生成AI中的扩散模型（如DALL-E 3、Stable Diffusion）和文本生成AI中的大语言模型（如ChatGPT、Gemini）进行技术概述，介绍其在医学辅助中的具体应用场景（如诊断报告生成与总结），并讨论基础模型的构建方法及其在医疗领域的应用，最后探讨基于国家数据和算力资源开发基础模型与医疗支持AI的路径。

Result: 论文系统梳理了生成式AI（扩散模型与LLM）及基础模型的技术原理和医疗应用现状，指出这些技术正在根本性地改变医疗AI的开发框架，并提出了利用国家层面数据与计算资源构建医疗基础模型的战略方向。

Conclusion: 生成式AI与基础模型正在重塑医疗AI开发范式。未来应充分整合国家数据和计算资源，构建面向医疗领域的专用基础模型，从而推动高性能医疗支持AI的发展。

Abstract: In recent years, generative AI has attracted significant public attention, and its use has been rapidly expanding across a wide range of domains. From creative tasks such as text summarization, idea generation, and source code generation, to the streamlining of medical support tasks like diagnostic report generation and summarization, AI is now deeply involved in many areas. Today's breadth of AI applications is clearly distinct from what was seen before generative AI gained widespread recognition. Representative generative AI services include DALL-E 3 (OpenAI, California, USA) and Stable Diffusion (Stability AI, London, England, UK) for image generation, ChatGPT (OpenAI, California, USA), and Gemini (Google, California, USA) for text generation. The rise of generative AI has been influenced by advances in deep learning models and the scaling up of data, models, and computational resources based on the scaling laws. Moreover, the emergence of foundation models, which are trained on large-scale datasets and possess general-purpose knowledge applicable to various downstream tasks, is creating a new paradigm in AI development. These shifts brought about by generative AI and foundation models also profoundly impact medical image processing, fundamentally changing the framework for AI development in healthcare. This paper provides an overview of diffusion models used in image generation AI and large language models (LLMs) used in text generation AI, and introduces their applications in medical support. This paper also discusses foundation models, which are gaining attention alongside generative AI, including their construction methods and applications in the medical field. Finally, the paper explores how to develop foundation models and high-performance AI for medical support by fully utilizing national data and computational resources.

</details>


### [173] [Entity-Aware Sequence Transduction for Player-Centric Ball Action Spotting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01696)
*Ruifeng Wang,Di Yang,Jiangtao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ME-DST（Multi-Entity Denoising Sequence Transduction），一种面向球员中心球类事件定位的多实体去噪序列转导方法。通过在整个编码过程中保留角色槽（role-slot）维度，采用时间注意力建模每个角色槽的历史演化、空间注意力在不同角色槽间交换信息，ME-DST在FOOTPASS数据集上达到0.778的Micro F1，比最强的官方TAAD+DST基线提升10.3个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有去噪序列转导（DST）基线将球员角色维度展平为帧级表示的一部分，削弱了建模球员特定时间演化与球员间交互的归纳偏置，导致对球员中心球类事件的时序精确定位与角色归属能力不足。

Method: 提出ME-DST，在编码全过程保留角色槽维度：①时间注意力建模每个角色槽的历史演化；②空间注意力在每帧跨角色槽交换信息；③引入可学习角色嵌入、基于跟踪的战术特征，以及X3D-L与Swin3D-S的融合视觉预测。该分解式设计明确分离球员内部演化与球员间上下文。

Result: 在FOOTPASS数据集上，ME-DST达到Micro F1为0.778，较最强官方TAAD+DST基线提升10.3个百分点。受控消融实验表明，保留实体轴和编码角色身份是性能提升的核心因素。

Conclusion: 显式的实体建模是球员中心体育事件理解的有效归纳偏置，为拥挤、部分遮挡的多智能体体育场景中的时序事件检测与角色归属提供了有力的解决方案。

Abstract: Player-centric ball action spotting requires temporally precise event detection together with actor attribution in crowded, partially observed multi-agent sports videos. Existing Denoising Sequence Transduction (DST) baselines treat the player-role dimension as part of a flattened frame-level representation, which weakens the inductive bias for modeling player-specific temporal evolution and inter-player interactions. To address this limitation, we propose Multi-Entity Denoising Sequence Transduction (ME-DST). ME-DST keeps the role-slot dimension throughout encoding. It uses temporal attention to model the history of each role slot, and spatial attention to exchange information across role slots at each frame. This factorized design gives the model a direct structure for separating within-player evolution from inter-player context. We also add learnable role embeddings, tracking-derived tactical features, and fused visual predictions from X3D-L and Swin3D-S. Experiments on the FOOTPASS dataset show that ME-DST reaches a Micro F1 of 0.778. This improves the strongest official TAAD+DST baseline by 10.3 percentage points. Controlled ablations show that preserving the entity axis and encoding role identity are central to this gain. These results suggest that explicit entity modeling is an effective inductive bias for player-centric sports event understanding.

</details>


### [174] [UniSim-SLAM: Feed-Forward SLAM with Unified Sim(3) Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01706)
*Inha Lee,Dongjae Jeong,Junhee Lee,Kyungdon Joo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniSim-SLAM是一个集成系统，采用轻量级双视角关键帧跟踪作为前端、周期性多视角子图细化作为后端，通过统一的Sim(3)多层级因子图联合优化全局关键帧位姿和子图位姿，解决了几何基础模型中输入视图依赖性导致的几何不一致和轨迹漂移问题。在TUM RGB-D和7-Scenes数据集上，在无标定设置下分别将轨迹误差降低38.5%和45.9%，达到最先进精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的几何基础模型支持SLAM的贪婪前馈推断，但其预测强烈依赖于输入视图集合，导致几何不一致；当预测结果在长序列上链接时会引起轨迹漂移。同时在线部署中，两视图跟踪的低延迟与多视图推断的约束丰富性之间存在权衡矛盾。

Method: 提出UniSim-SLAM集成系统：前端运行轻量级双视角关键帧跟踪（保证低延迟），后端执行周期性多视角子图细化（提供丰富约束）。由于组合预测定义在异构局部坐标且尺度不一致，设计统一的Sim(3)多层级因子图，联合优化全局关键帧位姿与子图位姿。该因子图融合三类边：时间维度的视图到视图里程计边、带深度统计尺度锚定的视图到子图桥接边、以及子图间串联与尺度约束边，以强制各子图间保持一致的相似变换关系。

Result: 在TUM RGB-D和7-Scenes数据集上的实验表明，UniSim-SLAM在无标定设置下达到最先进的精度，相比此前最优结果，TUM RGB-D上轨迹误差降低38.5%，7-Scenes上降低45.9%。

Conclusion: UniSim-SLAM通过前端的双视角轻量跟踪与后端的多视图子图细化相结合，并利用统一的Sim(3)多层级因子图协调异构局部坐标与尺度，有效缓解了几何基础模型在SLAM中的几何不一致和轨迹漂移，显著提升了无标定场景下的定位精度。

Abstract: Recent geometric foundation models enable feed-forward inference for SLAM, but their predictions are strongly dependent on the input view set, which leads to geometric inconsistencies and trajectory drift when results are chained over long sequences. Online deployment further exposes a trade-off between the low latency of two-view tracking and the constraint richness of multi-view inference. We introduce UniSim-SLAM, an integrated system that runs lightweight two-view keyframe tracking in the frontend and performs periodic multi-view submap refinement in the backend. To combine predictions defined in heterogeneous local coordinates with inconsistent scales, we formulate a unified multi-level factor graph on $Sim(3)$ that jointly optimizes global keyframe poses and submap poses. The graph integrates temporal view-to-view odometry edges, view-to-submap bridge edges with depth-statistics scale anchoring, and submap-to-submap tie and scale constraints to enforce consistent similarity relations across submaps. Experiments on TUM RGB-D and 7-Scenes show that UniSim-SLAM achieves state-of-the-art accuracy in the uncalibrated setting, reducing trajectory error by $38.5\% $ on TUM RGB-D and $45.9\%$ on 7-Scenes compared to prior best results. Project page: https://vision3d-lab.github.io/unisim-slam/

</details>


### [175] [SpatialQuery: Benchmarking Geometry-Grounded Multi-Instance Spatial Reasoning in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01709)
*Hai Nguyen,Tung Vu,Cong Tran*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SPATIALQUERY，一个免训练的视觉语言模型（VLM）空间推理框架，用于解决单张RGB图像中的最近实例距离查询（CIDQ）问题。通过Scene Cubifying将实例级度量几何转换为规范鸟瞰图，并结合不确定性感知链式推理（UA-CoT）提示，在SPATIALQUERY-1M基准（200个室内场景、超百万问答对）上，以Qwen3-VL-8B达到0.259 m的Floor-MAE、90.5%的Unc-Acc@0.3m和84.18%的近邻判断准确率，超越微调的空间专家模型、通用VLM和闭源前沿模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型在语义理解上表现优异，但在度量空间推理上不可靠，特别是当查询需要比较同一物体类别的多个实例时（如找出离参考物体最近的同类物体并估计距离）。现有模型难以解决此类细粒度的实例级空间推理问题。

Method: SPATIALQUERY框架包含两大核心组件：(1) Scene Cubifying——将实例级度量几何从单张RGB图像中恢复并转换为规范的鸟瞰图表示，将物体表示为统一大小、按类别编码的方块以强调其相对地面平面位置；(2) Uncertainty-Aware Chain-of-Thought (UA-CoT)提示——将从几何中推导出的逐实例不确定性信息融入VLM推理过程中，引导模型进行更可靠的空间推理。

Result: 在SPATIALQUERY-1M基准上，SPATIALQUERY搭配Qwen3-VL-8B无需任务特定微调或架构修改即达到Floor-MAE为0.259 m，Unc-Acc@0.3m为90.5%，近邻决策准确率为84.18%，在所有指标上均优于微调的空间专家模型、通用VLM以及闭源前沿模型。

Conclusion: SPATIALQUERY证明了将底层度量几何显式编码为规范鸟瞰表示并注入不确定性信息，可以有效弥补VLM在实例级空间推理上的不足，而无需额外的训练或架构改动，为VLM驱动的空间感知提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) achieve strong semantic understanding but remain unreliable in metric spatial reasoning, particularly when queries require comparing multiple instances of the same object category. We study this problem through the Closest-Instance Distance Query (CIDQ), where a model must identify the nearest visible candidate to a unique reference object and estimate their gravity-aligned floor-plane distance. We introduce SPATIALQUERY, a training- free framework for CIDQ reasoning from a single RGB image, together with SPATIALQUERY-1M, a benchmark containing over one million RGB-only question-answer pairs from 200 indoor scenes. SPATIALQUERY recovers instance-level metric geometry and transforms it into a canonical Bird's-Eye View through Scene Cubifying, which represents objects as uniformly sized, category-coded blocks to emphasize their relative floor- plane locations. We further propose Uncertainty-Aware Chain-of-Thought (UA-CoT) prompting, which incorporates geometry- derived per-instance uncertainty into the VLM reasoning process. Without task-specific fine-tuning or architectural modification, SPATIALQUERY with Qwen3-VL-8B achieves a Floor-MAE of 0.259 m, an Unc-Acc@0.3 m of 90.5%, and a proximity-decision accuracy of 84.18%, outperforming fine-tuned spatial specialists, general-purpose VLMs, and closed-source frontier models. Code, benchmark resources, and an interactive demo are available at https://namhai1810.github.io/SpatialQuery/.

</details>


### [176] [STC-Net: Electroluminescence-Based Solar Cell Crack Segmentation for Power Loss Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01714)
*Shanaka Ramesh Gunasekara,Akila Eranda Devanarayana,Imasha Guruge,Nuwantha Fernando,Ehsan Asadi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出STC-Net，一种融合边缘先验、光谱先验和边界-拓扑精细化模块的太阳能电池裂纹分割网络，并通过裂纹相关非活性区域代理将分割扩展到功率损失估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光伏EL图像中的裂纹缺陷具有细长、延展且受结构约束的特点，现有分割方法难以准确捕获这些裂纹的连续性和边界信息，进而影响光伏可靠性分析。

Method: STC-Net包含三个核心组件：边缘先验（edge priors）用于增强裂纹边界信息、光谱先验（spectral priors）用于突出裂纹的结构特征、边界-拓扑精细化模块用于改善裂纹的连续性和边界保持。此外，框架通过从预测掩膜推导裂纹相关的非活性区域代理，将分割结果扩展到功率损失估计。

Result: 在PVEL-S数据集上，STC-Net训练阶段达到95.98 MIoU、98.01 MDice和98.00 MAcc；在未见测试样本上达到72.52 MIoU和80.16 MDice。

Conclusion: STC-Net能够提供准确的裂纹定位，并在EL缺陷分割与光伏退化评估之间建立了实用的桥梁。

Abstract: Accurate crack assessment in electroluminescence (EL) images is important for photovoltaic (PV) reliability analysis, yet existing segmentation methods often fail to capture the thin, elongated, and structurally constrained nature of crack defects. This paper proposes a Solar Topology Crack Network (STC-Net) that incorporates edge priors, spectral priors, and a boundary-topology refinement module to improve crack continuity and boundary preservation. The framework further extends segmentation to power-loss estimation by deriving a crack-associated inactive-area proxy from the predicted masks. Experiments on the PVEL-S dataset show that STC-Net achieves 95.98 MIoU, 98.01 MDice, and 98.00 MAcc during training, and 72.52 MIoU and 80.16 MDice on unseen test samples. These results demonstrate that STC-Net provides accurate crack localization while offering a practical link between EL-based defect segmentation and PV degradation assessment.

</details>


### [177] [When Extreme Darkness Meets Motion Blur: MeanFlow for Unified RAW Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01720)
*Zepu Wang,Jingze Liang,Weijie Xiao,Kexin Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个针对真实采集退化（尤其是运动退化）下极端低光RAW增强的统一框架，包括新的数据集SIDED、统一的RAW分词器、MeanFlow增强模块和物理引导的细化模型，在耦合运动与噪声退化下达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有极端低光RAW增强方法主要关注光照和噪声，忽略了实际低光成像中固有的运动退化（如相机抖动、物体运动），导致在实际场景中性能受限。

Method: 1) 构建SIDED数据集，在极端低光RAW对上施加受控运动退化同时保留原始传感器噪声；2) 提出统一RAW分词器，配备显式域条件表示校准，对齐极端低光与正常曝光RAW数据；3) 提出MeanFlow在单次函数评估中完成增强；4) 引入物理引导的细化模型，强化光照-反射一致性、像素保真度和颜色保持，且不增加推理成本。

Result: 大量实验表明该框架在极端低光RAW增强任务上达到最先进性能，并能稳健处理运动与噪声耦合退化。

Conclusion: 这是首个将极端低光RAW增强形式化为真实运动退化采集场景并用MeanFlow解决的工作，验证了在复杂真实退化条件下增强的有效性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Extremely low-light RAW enhancement aims to recover severely attenuated sensor signals, yet existing methods often focus on illumination and noise while overlooking the motion-induced degradations inherent in practical low-light imaging. We present a framework for robust extremely low-light RAW enhancement under realistic acquisition degradations. First, we introduce See in the Degraded Extremely Dark (SIDED), a new dataset that applies controlled motion degradation to extremely low-light RAW pairs while retaining their original sensor noise. Second, we propose a unified RAW tokenizer equipped with explicit domain-conditioned representation calibration to align extremely low-light and well-exposed RAW data, followed by a MeanFlow that performs enhancement in a single function evaluation. To our knowledge, this is the first work to formulate extremely low-light RAW enhancement under realistic motion-degraded acquisition and address it with MeanFlow. We further introduce a physics-guided refinement model to strengthen illumination--reflectance consistency, pixel fidelity, and color preservation without incurring additional inference cost. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our framework achieves state-of-the-art performance in extremely low-light RAW enhancement, and robustly handles coupled motion and noise degradations.

</details>


### [178] [G-Skin: Learning to Bind 3D Gaussians with Generative Visual Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01726)
*Yuxin Yao,Kendong Liu,Shiqi Zhou,Jiazhi Xia,Junhui Hou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: G-Skin是一个用于3D高斯表示的可表达、高保真动画的生成式蒙皮框架。它通过引入骨架可控的图像生成模型，利用2D视觉基础模型将强运动先验蒸馏为伪引导，并配合几何感知正则化的优化流程，解决了3D高斯蒙皮数据稀缺的问题，在动画质量上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D高斯泼溅在真实感高效渲染方面取得了显著成功，导致以3D高斯基元表示的3D资产迅速增长。直接对这些资产进行任意骨架拓扑的绑定非常理想，但训练前馈蒙皮框架因缺乏高质量3D高斯绑定数据集而不可行；迁移基于网格的技术到3D高斯表示也存在问题，因为3D高斯基元不受限于表面且缺乏显式拓扑连接，同时这类方法对训练数据依赖强、泛化能力差，而获取高质量绑定数据又极其昂贵。

Method: G-Skin提出一个生成式蒙皮框架：首先引入一个骨架可控的图像生成模型，利用2D视觉基础模型将强大的运动先验蒸馏为伪引导，以克服3D数据稀缺问题；然后在这些先验的引导下，构建一个包含几何感知正则化的优化流程，稳定学习过程并确保蒙皮权重平滑且结构连贯；此外，该框架还灵活泛化到3D高斯表示的增强变体，以缓解动画引起的渲染伪影。

Result: 大量实验验证了G-Skin的有效性，在动画质量和表达力上均展现出对现有最先进方法的明显优势。

Conclusion: G-Skin通过生成式范式结合2D先验蒸馏与几何感知正则化，成功解决了3D高斯蒙皮数据稀缺问题，实现了对3D高斯资产的高保真、可表达动画绑定，并展示了良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting has achieved remarkable success in photorealistic and efficient rendering, leading to a rapid increase in 3D assets represented by 3D Gaussian primitives. Directly rigging these assets with arbitrary skeleton topologies is highly desirable. However, training a feed-forward skinning framework is infeasible due to the lack of high-quality 3D Gaussian rigging datasets. An alternative solution is to transfer mesh-based techniques to 3D Gaussian-based representation, but 3D Gaussian primitives are not restricted to the surface and lack explicit topological connectivity. Moreover, this kind of method suffers from poor generalization to unseen data due to its strong dependence on training data, while acquiring high-quality rigging data is prohibitively expensive. To address this challenging problem, we propose G-Skin, a novel generative skinning framework designed for expressive and high-fidelity animation with 3D Gaussian representation. To overcome this 3D data scarcity, we introduce a skeleton-controllable image generation model leveraging 2D vision foundation models to distill powerful motion priors into pseudo-guidance. Guided by these priors, we formulate an optimization pipeline incorporating geometry-aware regularizations, which stabilizes the learning process and ensures smooth, structurally coherent skinning weights. G-Skin also generalizes flexibly to the augmented variants of 3D Gaussian representation designed to mitigate animation-induced rendering artifacts. Extensive experiments validate the effectiveness of our approach, demonstrating clear advantages over state-of-the-art methods. Project page: https://yaoyx689.github.io/GSkin.html.

</details>


### [179] [Learning Where to Look and How to Judge: Resolution-agnostic Image Quality Assessment with Quality-aware Saliency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01730)
*Hakan Emre Gedik,Shashank Gupta,Alan Bovik*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReLIQS是一个基于CLIP的多尺度补丁驱动架构的无参考图像质量评估（NR IQA）模型，它通过在多个分辨率（包括原始分辨率）上采样固定大小的补丁，利用轻量级感知重要性估计器选择信息丰富的补丁，并通过潜在质量轴模块聚合嵌入生成图像级质量分数。该模型具备分辨率无关性、保留原始质量线索、可跨多个主观研究联合训练、计算高效且预算自适应等特性，在真实、合成和AIGC基准上优于强CNN、CLIP和MLLM基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NR IQA系统存在多方面缺陷：激进resizing丢弃关键质量线索、无法跨分辨率泛化、无法在具有不匹配MOS尺度的异构IQA数据集上联合训练、或计算开销过大。许多SOTA系统至少违反了一项基本要求。

Method: ReLIQS采用CLIP-based多尺度补丁驱动架构：1) 在多个分辨率（包括原始分辨率）上采样固定大小补丁，用CLIP视觉骨干编码；2) 轻量级Perceptual Importance Estimator预测IQA特定重要性图，选择少量信息丰富补丁；3) Latent Quality Axis Module聚合补丁嵌入为单一图像级分数。该模型同时学习"看哪里"和"如何判断质量"，并支持多数据集联合训练与计算预算自适应。

Result: 在涵盖不同分辨率和失真的真实、合成及AIGC基准上，ReLIQS以匹配或更低的计算成本，比强CNN、CLIP和MLLM基线取得了更好的泛化性能。

Conclusion: ReLIQS通过多尺度补丁采样和重要性估计机制，同时满足分辨率无关、保留原始质量线索、多数据集联合训练和计算高效等所有基本要求，为NR IQA提供了一种兼顾泛化能力与计算效率的解决方案。

Abstract: No-reference image quality assessment (NR IQA) has recently benefited from deep and multimodal models, yet many SOTA systems still violate at least one basic requirement: they either discard critical quality cues via aggressive resizing, fail to generalize across resolutions, cannot be jointly trained on heterogeneous IQA datasets with mismatched MOS scales, or require prohibitive computation. We present \textbf{ReLIQS}, a model for \textbf{Re}solution-agnostic \textbf{L}earning for \textbf{I}mage \textbf{Q}uality with \textbf{S}aliency, which is resolution-agnostic, preserves original-resolution quality cues, learns from multiple subjective studies, and remains computationally efficient and budget-adaptive. ReLIQS is a CLIP-based multiscale patch-driven architecture that learns both \emph{where to look} and \emph{how to judge} quality. Fixed-size patches are sampled across multiple resolutions, including the original resolution, and encoded with a CLIP vision backbone. A lightweight Perceptual Importance Estimator then predicts IQA-specific importance maps to select a small set of informative patches, and a Latent Quality Axis Module aggregates their embeddings into a single image-level score. Across authentic, synthetic, and AIGC benchmarks spanning diverse resolutions and distortions, ReLIQS generalizes better than strong CNN-, CLIP-, and MLLM-based baselines with matching or reduced computational cost.

</details>


### [180] [IDraw: Artist Verification from Digital Drawing Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01737)
*Nayoung Kim,Nan Jiang,Bangjie Sun,Jaewon Shin,Sojeong Kim,Jun Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出IDraw框架，通过从完成图像中推断艺术家绘画行为（如笔压、移动速度）并抑制绘画内容干扰，实现数字绘画作者验证。在首次构建的多模态数据集（1,110幅画作、37位艺术家、14种平板笔传感器信号）上，IDraw在未知艺术家和九种图像编码器上始终优于标准方法，验证错误率最高降低40%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字绘画在线分享日益普遍，作者验证对保护艺术家和解决纠纷至关重要。当作者身份存疑时，验证只能依赖争议画作和已知参考画作，但存在两大挑战：一是艺术家特有的绘画行为（如笔压、速度）信息无法从已完成画作中获得；二是描绘对象或场景的相似性会掩盖艺术家风格的相似性。

Method: IDraw框架：1）从配对平板笔传感器信号训练的分离艺术家中学习，从完成图像中推断绘画行为，无需被验证艺术家的传感器数据；2）通过识别同一对象在不同艺术家画作间共享的信息并加以抑制，减少绘画内容的干扰；3）构建首个数字绘画作者验证的多模态数据集，包含1,110幅画作、37位艺术家和14种平板笔传感器信号。

Result: 在未见过的艺术家上跨九种图像编码器骨干网络评估，IDraw始终优于标准基于图像的验证方法，验证错误率最多降低40%。

Conclusion: 从完成图像中推断绘画行为并抑制绘画内容，显著提升了数字绘画作者验证的准确性。

Abstract: As digital drawings are increasingly shared online, reliable authorship verification has become important for protecting artists and resolving disputes. Yet when authorship is questioned, verification may have to rely only on the disputed drawing and reference drawings known to be created by the claimed artist. This setting is challenging for two reasons. First, artist-specific drawing behavior, such as pen pressure and movement speed, is informative but is not available from a completed drawing. Second, similarities in the depicted object or scene can obscure similarities arising from the artist. We propose IDraw, a framework that learns from drawings paired with tablet-pen sensor signals collected from separate training artists. This allows IDraw to infer drawing behavior from completed images during a later authorship dispute, without requiring sensor data from the artist being verified. IDraw also reduces the influence of drawing content by identifying information shared by drawings of the same object across different artists and suppressing it before comparing drawings. To support this approach, we construct the first multimodal dataset for digital drawing authorship verification, containing 1,110 drawings from 37 artists and 14 types of tablet-pen sensor signals. Evaluated on previously unseen artists across nine image-encoder backbones, IDraw consistently outperforms standard image-based verification and reduces verification error by up to 40%. These results demonstrate that inferring drawing behavior from completed images and suppressing drawing content improve digital drawing authorship verification.

</details>


### [181] [SPECTRA: Band-Routed Embedding and Stage-Wise LoRA for Cross-Sensor Fine-Tuning of Geospatial Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01751)
*Xingyan Li,Jordan A. Caraballo-Vega,Jie Gong,Mark L. Carroll,Jianwu Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SPECTRA是一个面向地理空间基础模型（GeoFMs）的参数高效微调框架，同时解决光谱不匹配问题和适应成本问题。它通过Band-Routed Embedding（BRE）将所有可用的下游波段映射到预训练模型期望的波段空间，并通过Stage-wise Transferability-aware LoRA（ST-LoRA）在微调前估计各阶段的迁移性并分配不同的LoRA秩，从而在不改变预训练补丁嵌入接口的前提下利用全部光谱波段，同时减少可训练参数量。在三个EO预训练GeoFMs和四个下游分割数据集上的实验表明，BRE利用全部光谱波段提升了性能，ST-LoRA相比完整微调和标准LoRA减少了可训练参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GeoFMs适配实际下游数据面临两个关键挑战：一是光谱不匹配问题——预训练补丁嵌入期望固定的输入波段，而下游传感器可能提供不同的通道；二是微调成本高昂且效率低下。虽然已有工作分别针对这两个挑战进行了尝试，但如何在光谱不匹配条件下联合提升微调性能并降低适配成本仍未被充分探索。

Method: SPECTRA框架包含两个核心组件：1）Band-Routed Embedding（BRE），将所有可用的下游波段映射到预训练GeoFM所期望的波段空间，从而在不修改预训练补丁嵌入接口的前提下充分利用下游数据集中的全部光谱波段；2）Stage-wise Transferability-aware LoRA（ST-LoRA）微调方法，在正式微调前先估计各层级的迁移性，据此为不同阶段分配不同的LoRA秩，将可训练参数集中于对目标任务迁移性高的阶段。

Result: 在三个EO预训练GeoFMs和四个下游分割数据集上的实验结果表明：BRE通过利用全部光谱波段提升了模型性能；ST-LoRA相比完整微调和标准LoRA减少了可训练参数的数量。代码已开源。

Conclusion: SPECTRA在统一框架内同时解决了光谱不匹配与适配成本两个问题，为GeoFMs在具有不同光谱配置的下游实际数据上的高效适配提供了有效的解决方案，兼具性能提升与参数效率。

Abstract: Geospatial foundation models (GeoFMs), pretrained on large-scale geospatial data such as Earth observation (EO), climate, and weather data, have shown promising performance when fine-tuned on diverse downstream tasks. However, there are two challenges of adapting EO-pretrained GeoFMs to practical downstream datasets. The first challenge is how to handle spectral mismatch: pretrained patch embeddings expect a fixed set of input bands, whereas downstream sensors may provide different channels. The second challenge is how to reduce fine-tuning cost and make it efficient. While existing work has made efforts on these challenges individually, jointly improving fine-tuning performance under spectral mismatch while reducing adaptation cost remains underexplored. We propose SPECTRA, a parameter-efficient fine-tuning framework that addresses both spectral mismatch and adaptation cost. To handle spectral mismatch, SPECTRA introduces Band-Routed Embedding (BRE), which maps all available downstream bands into the band space expected by the pretrained GeoFM. By using BRE, all available bands in the downstream dataset are utilized to improve the selected-band input without changing the pretrained patch embedding interface. To reduce adaptation cost, SPECTRA further introduces a Stage-wise Transferability-aware LoRA (ST-LoRA) fine-tuning. ST-LoRA estimates stage-wise transferability before fine-tuning and assigns stage-specific LoRA ranks, concentrating trainable parameters on the stages with high transferability for the target task. Across three EO-pretrained GeoFMs and four downstream segmentation datasets, experiments show that BRE improves performance by utilizing all spectral bands, while ST-LoRA reduces trainable parameters compared with full fine-tuning and standard LoRA. Code is available at https://github.com/big-data-lab-umbc/SPECTRA.

</details>


### [182] [Can Urban Blight Be Accessed with Vision-language Models: A Case Study in Detroit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01753)
*Xiaohao Yang,Aohua Tian,Derek Van Berkel,Xu Qiang,Mark Lindquist*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种利用开源大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）和多视图图像评估住宅衰败（residential blight）的可扩展框架，通过结构化提示词指导模型评估屋顶完整性、墙体损坏和门窗破损等住房属性，并采用基于XGBoost的集成堆叠方法提升评估精度。结果显示多视图可提高准确性、不同模型有差异化推理优势、集成学习优于单一基模型，为传统衰败调查提供了低成本、可定期更新的替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统住宅衰败调查成本高昂、周期长，难以在大规模范围内持续维护。评估城市衰败对于指导城市规划、精准改造和保障公共健康至关重要，因此亟需一种低成本、可扩展且可定期更新的评估方法。

Method: 提出一个可扩展框架：使用开源大型视觉语言模型对多视角图像进行住宅衰败评估。通过结构化提示词引导模型评估屋顶完整性、墙体损坏、门窗破损等住房属性，同时输出二值判断和破损概率估计。评估性能时与专业人工标注对比，测试多个LVLM模型，并构建基于XGBoost的集成堆叠方法和加权评分系统。

Result: 研究结果表明：(i) 多视角街道图像可提升评估精度；(ii) 不同大型视觉语言模型具有差异化的推理优势；(iii) 基于XGBoost的集成学习器优于各基础模型，在所有住宅条件和衰败评估场景下均表现出更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该方法可实现低成本、可定期更新的住房存量状况追踪与管理，作为传统衰败调查的有效补充手段，具备实际应用价值。

Abstract: Addressing urban blight has seen increased focus in the past 15 years. Assessing urban blight is essential for guiding urban planning, targeting rehabilitation, and safeguarding public health, yet traditional residential blight surveys are difficult to maintain at scale due to the labor-intensive cost and long-term cycle. This study introduced a scalable framework for estimating residential blight using open-source large vision-language models on multiple views. Structured prompts guided models to evaluate housing attributes, including roof integrity, wall damage, and broken or boarded openings, producing both binary assessments and probabilistic estimates of disrepair. To evaluate the performance of these visual assessments, we compared professional human annotations of these features across several models, including an ensemble stacking approach based on XGBoost and a weighted scoring system. Results showed that (i) multiple street views can contribute to the improvement of accuracy, (ii) large vision-language models have different strengths of inference, (iii) the ensemble learner outperforms individual base models, enhancing robustness across all residential conditions and blight assessment. The practical application of the method allows low-cost tracking and management of housing stock conditions, providing a regularly updatable complement to traditional blight surveys.

</details>


### [183] [Pixel Ignores, Superpixel Sees: Adverse Weather Image Restoration via Semantic-Center SSM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01760)
*Dayu Li,Shihao Zhou,Leizhi Shu,Jin Wu,Chi Man Vong,Jufeng Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SSR，一种基于语义中心引导的状态空间模型用于恶劣天气图像恢复。核心思想是将传统的像素序列扫描策略转变为语义引导的扫描方式，通过超像素聚类划分感知一致的区域，并在语义相关区域内进行关系建模。在6个基准数据集上超越了现有最先进方法，同时保持有竞争力的计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有恶劣天气图像恢复方法通过建模像素间关系来解决问题，但这种范式忽略了退化在空间上的非均匀性，且会在语义冲突区域学习到非判别性特征，导致恢复效果不佳。

Method: 提出SSR框架，包含两个核心模块：(1) 超像素引导的选择性扫描机制（S3M），通过超像素聚类将图像划分为感知一致的区域，并在语义相关区域内进行关系建模；(2) 区域级门控机制（RGM），沿通道维度在每个语义超像素单元内调制退化异常值，实现区域内校准。

Result: 在6个公认的基准数据集上进行大量实验，SSR在恢复性能上优于最先进模型，同时保持有竞争力的计算成本。

Conclusion: 通过将扫描策略从像素序列转为语义引导，并引入超像素级区域建模与门控校准，SSR有效解决了恶劣天气下退化空间非均匀性问题，在性能和效率之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: Adverse weather image restoration aims to recover clear visibility from degraded images in complex weather conditions.
  Existing works attempt to address this problem by modeling relationships between pixels, however, this paradigm defies the spatially non-uniformity fact of degradations and learns non-discriminative features from semantic-conflict regions.
  In this paper, we propose SSR, a \textbf{S}emantic-center guilded \textbf{S}tate space model for image \textbf{R}estoration.
  The key idea of SSR is to shift the conventional scanning strategy of pixel-serial to semantic-guilded one.
  Specifically, we introduce a Superpixel-guided Selective Scan Mechanism ($\text{S}^3$M), which first partitions the image into perceptually coherent regions via superpixel clustering and then performs relations modeling within the semantic-related regions.
  Moreover, a Region-level Gating Mechanism (RGM) is developed to perform intra-region calibration by modulating degradation outliers within each semantic superpixel unit along the channel dimension.
  Extensive experiments on \textbf{6} well-established benchmarks demonstrate that SSR performs favorably against state-of-the-art models with competitive computational cost.

</details>


### [184] [DecoupleGS: Interactive 3D Gaussian Splatting for End-to-End Autonomous Driving Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01761)
*Siying Li,Ying Ni,Jie Sun,Jian Sun,Haotian Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DecoupleGS，一种面向大规模端到端自动驾驶评估的解耦式三维高斯泼溅（3DGS）框架，将场景分解为高保真静态背景与可操控动态智能体，通过资产压缩、地图引导几何配准和代理重光照三个模块，实现兼具视觉保真度、交互性和实时性的闭环仿真验证平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端自动驾驶算法需要在具备高视觉保真度、强交互性和实时性的仿真环境中进行严格的闭环验证。现有方法（从游戏引擎到静态神经渲染）在这些需求之间存在固有取舍，且难以实现端到端测试所必需的动态场景组合能力。

Method: DecoupleGS采用解耦式架构：将场景本质拆分为高保真静态背景和可操控的动态智能体（基于对象中心规范表示），并提出三个针对性模块解决表示冲突：(1) 通过感知剪枝和向量量化实现资产压缩，以支持实时交通渲染；(2) 基于语义拓扑的地图引导几何配准，严格对齐轨迹；(3) 基于代理的重光照传递环境光照，实现无缝光度集成。

Result: 大量实验表明，DecoupleGS在保真度与效率之间取得了平衡，改善了度量一致性和光度一致性，并为端到端自动驾驶评估提供了一个实用的闭环传感器仿真平台。

Conclusion: DecoupleGS通过解耦场景表示和针对性模块设计，有效弥合了仿真精度与动态交互性之间的鸿沟，为端到端自动驾驶的大规模闭环评估提供了可行的仿真基础设施。

Abstract: End-to-end (E2E) autonomous driving algorithms require rigorous closed-loop validation in simulation environments offering high visual fidelity, strong interactivity, and real-time performance. Existing approaches, from game engines to static neural rendering, inherently trade off these requirements and struggle with the dynamic scene composition essential for E2E testing. To bridge this gap, we propose a novel decoupled 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) framework tailored for large-scale E2E evaluation. We fundamentally decompose scenes into a high-fidelity static background and manipulable dynamic agents using an object-centric canonical representation. To resolve resulting representational conflicts, we introduce three targeted modules: (1) asset compression via perceptual pruning and vector quantization for real-time traffic rendering; (2) map-guided geometric registration leveraging semantic topology to strictly align trajectories; and (3) proxy-based relighting transferring ambient illumination for seamless photometric integration. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DecoupleGS achieves a balanced fidelity-efficiency trade-off, improves metric and photometric consistency, and provides a practical closed-loop sensor simulation platform for E2E autonomous driving evaluation.

</details>


### [185] [LiveLight: Real-time Streaming Video Relighting with Interactive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01771)
*Yue Ma,Jiangming Wang,Yucheng Wang,Xilai Wang,Zhiyuan Li,Xinyu Wang,Hongyu Liu,Ruofan Liang,Songchun Zhang,Yuxuan Xue,Qifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LiveLight是首个基于扩散模型的实时流式视频重光照框架，支持交互式3D光照控制。通过光照注入适配器、几何引导反馈分支和渐进滚动窗口策略三大设计，在极低NFE下实现高质量视频重光照，同时支持任意时长的流式交互，达到实时速度并显著优于离线基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时流式视频重光照需要同时克服三大挑战：如何将动态3D光照有效注入扩散模型、如何在极低NFE预算下保持高保真生成以实现实时速度、以及如何支持连续流式处理以满足交互式控制需求。现有方法无法同时满足这三方面的要求。

Method: 提出三个关键设计：（1）轻量级适配器将多平面光辐照度（MPLI）条件——深度感知的辐照度图编码3D光照几何——直接注入扩散主干；（2）几何引导反馈分支，利用冻结的几何估计器在训练时强制执行深度和法线一致的重光照，确保几何上合理的着色且不增加推理开销；（3）渐进滚动窗口策略，维护不同噪声级别的潜在块去噪阶梯，通过状态传播保证时间一致性并支持任意长度视频重光照和逐帧参考刷新。

Result: 在真实世界和合成基准上的大量实验表明，LiveLight在实时速度下实现了最先进的重光照质量，在时间稳定性、光照可控性和用户偏好方面显著优于离线基线。

Conclusion: LiveLight证明了扩散模型可以在极低NFE下实现实时、交互式、几何一致的流式视频重光照。研究将公开模型、训练数据和合成数据生成器以促进该领域研究。

Abstract: We present LiveLight, the first diffusion-based framework for real-time streaming video relighting with interactive 3D lighting control. Achieving this is non-trivial, as it requires overcoming three critical challenges: effectively injecting dynamic 3D lighting into a diffusion model, maintaining high-fidelity generation under an extremely low NFE (Number of Function Evaluations) budget for real-time speed, and facilitating continuous streaming for interactive control. To address these pain points, we propose three key designs. First, for accurate lighting injection, we propose a lightweight adapter that feeds Multi-Plane Light Irradiance (MPLI) conditions-depth-aware irradiance maps encoding 3D lighting geometry-directly into the diffusion backbone. Second, to prevent rendering quality degradation at low NFEs towards real-time distillation, we introduce a geometry-guided feedback branch. This training-time constraint leverages a frozen geometry estimator to enforce depth- and normal-consistent relighting, ensuring geometrically plausible shading without adding inference overhead. Finally, to enable streaming interaction, we develop a progressive rolling-window strategy that maintains a denoising ladder of latent chunks at varying noise levels. By propagating intermediate states, this strategy guarantees temporal coherence and supports arbitrarily long video relighting with per-frame reference refresh. Extensive experiments on real-world and synthetic benchmarks demonstrate that LiveLight achieves state-of-the-art relighting quality while running at real-time speed, significantly outperforming offline baselines in temporal stability, lighting controllability, and user preference. To foster real-time interactive relighting research, we will publicly release our models, training data, and synthetic data generator.

</details>


### [186] [Investigating Social Bias in Narrative Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01780)
*Junyeong Park,Sowon Min,Euna Jang,Soobin Kim,Jiho Jin,Hyunseung Lim,Gahyeon Bae,Hwajung Hong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了文本到图像（T2I）生成模型在照片、故事板和漫画三种视觉格式中的社会偏见表现，发现故事板（+9.6pp）和漫画（+18.2pp）生成中的偏见输出显著高于照片生成（25.9%），且叙事性格式通过事件序列、角色定位、叙事结局和文字元素更显式地暴露偏见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有T2I模型的偏见评估主要集中于照片生成任务，忽略了故事板和漫画等多面板叙事性视觉格式，因此缺乏对这些格式中偏见如何表现的理解。鉴于T2I模型已广泛应用于媒体创作和教育，探究不同视觉格式中的偏见差异至关重要。

Method: 作者将文本基础偏见评估框架BBG适配到图像生成任务，在六个T2I模型中系统比较了照片、故事板和漫画三种格式下的偏见表达方式，并进行定量统计和定性分析。

Result: 专有模型在照片生成中平均产生25.9%的偏见输出，故事板生成增加9.6个百分点，漫画生成增加18.2个百分点。照片中的偏见主要通过微妙视觉线索编码，而故事板和漫画通过事件序列、角色定位、叙事结局和文字元素更显式地暴露偏见。

Conclusion: 照片生成中不那么明显的偏见会在叙事性视觉格式中更突出地展现，强调了在照片生成之外用多样化视觉格式评估T2I系统的重要性。

Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) generation models are increasingly embedded in applications such as media content creation and education, raising concerns about how their outputs may reproduce social biases. Prior work has shown that T2I models exhibit social biases, yet existing evaluations largely focus on a photo generation task. As a result, it remains unclear whether and how such biases manifest in more narrative visual formats, such as storyboards and comics, where characters and events are presented across multiple panels. In this work, we compare bias expression across photo, storyboard, and comic generation in six T2I models by adapting BBG, a text-based bias evaluation framework, to image generation. Our results show that proprietary models generate 25.9% biased outputs in photo generation on average, with biased outputs increasing by 9.6pp in storyboard generation and 18.2pp in comic generation. We also find that photos mainly encode biases through subtle visual cues, while storyboards and comics reveal them more explicitly through event sequencing, character positioning, narrative resolution, and textual elements. These findings show that biases that remain less visible in photo generation may surface in narrative visual formats, highlighting the importance of evaluating T2I systems with diverse visual formats beyond photo generation.

</details>


### [187] [Parameter-Dynamic Adaptive Fusion and Calibration Network for RGBT Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01807)
*Zhaoding Ding,Chenglong Li,Jiandong Jin,Kewei Ying,Wentao Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PAFCNet，一种参数动态的自适应融合与校准网络，用于RGBT跟踪。通过目标自适应超网络（TA-HyperNet）动态生成目标条件化参数，实现多模态融合和时空校准，使其适应目标外观变化和模态质量波动。在多个RGBT跟踪基准上取得了具有竞争力的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RGBT跟踪器的融合函数在不同目标和场景下使用固定参数，尽管动态架构方法提高了融合灵活性，但仍无法将融合参数适应到不断变化的目标状态，导致跟踪性能受限。

Method: PAFCNet包含三个关键组件：1）目标自适应超网络（TA-HyperNet），利用模板表示（保留稳定目标身份和最近外观变化，且背景干扰少）生成目标条件化参数；2）目标感知的参数动态融合模块，使用生成的参数调制融合过程，适应目标外观变化和复杂场景；3）动态时空校准模块，利用TA-HyperNet生成校准参数用于时空token，在信息传播前动态校准历史信息，提高时间表示的可靠性。

Result: 在多个RGBT跟踪基准上，PAFCNet取得了具有竞争力的性能，验证了参数动态融合与校准的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过动态生成目标条件化的融合与校准参数，PAFCNet能够有效应对目标外观变化和模态质量波动，为RGBT跟踪提供了一种参数动态自适应的新思路。

Abstract: Existing RGBT trackers typically employ fusion functions with fixed parameters across different targets and scenarios. Although dynamic-architecture methods improve fusion flexibility by selecting among predefined operations, they still cannot adapt the fusion parameters to the evolving target state. To address these issues, we propose a Parameter-Dynamic Adaptive Fusion and Calibration Network (PAFCNet) for RGBT tracking. PAFCNet dynamically generates target-conditioned parameters for multimodal fusion and temporal calibration, enabling the tracking process to adapt to target appearance variations and modality quality fluctuations. Specifically, we introduce a Target-Adaptive Hypernetwork (TA-HyperNet) that leverages template representations, which preserve stable target identity and recent appearance changes with less background interference, to generate target-conditioned parameters for subsequent fusion and calibration. Based on TA-HyperNet, we design a target-aware parameter-dynamic fusion module that uses the generated parameters to modulate the fusion process. This enables the fusion module to adapt to changes in target appearance and complex scene conditions. Furthermore, since spatio-temporal information propagation may accumulate tracking noise, we propose a dynamic spatio-temporal calibration module that employs TA-HyperNet to generate calibration parameters for spatio-temporal tokens. By dynamically calibrating historical information before propagation, the module improves the reliability of temporal representations. Experimental results demonstrate that PAFCNet achieves competitive performance on multiple RGBT tracking benchmarks.

</details>


### [188] [SecondOpinion: Anatomy-Aware Gated Reasoning for Efficient Medical Image Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01808)
*Siam Tahsin Bhuiyan,Rashedur Rahman,Sefatul Wasi,Riyadul Islam,Syoji Kobashi,Ashraful Islam,Saadia Binte Alam*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SecondOpinion框架，通过一个快速主流处理所有病例，同时由一个显式训练为二分类正确性分类器的GateKeeper门控机制判断是否需要调用第二个解剖引导流进行"二次意见"处理。在胸部X光和骨盆骨折数据集上，该方法达到或超越现有SOTA性能，同时仅激活解剖流于9.23%的胸部X光病例、24.12%的可见骨折和45.71%的不可见骨折病例，激活率与任务难度直接相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习模型对所有输入施加固定计算量，不区分病例难度；解剖引导双流架构虽能提升诊断性能，但对所有病例无条件评估两个流，即使在单一流已能高置信度解决的情况下也浪费计算资源。需要一种机制来按需分配解剖推理资源，类似于临床医生对疑难病例寻求二次意见的做法。

Method: 提出SecondOpinion框架，包含：(1) 一个快速主流处理所有病例；(2) GateKeeper门控机制，被显式训练为二元正确性分类器，判断主流预测是否需要额外审阅；(3) 当激活时，通过轻量级交叉注意力融合模块将两个流结合起来。门控信号受正确性监督而非无监督置信度。

Result: 在统一五类胸部X光数据集和骨盆骨折数据集（含在X光上不可见但经CT确认的更难的held-out子集）上，SecondOpinion达到或超越先前SOTA性能。解剖引导流仅在9.23%的胸部X光病例、24.12%的可见骨折和45.71%的不可见骨折病例中被激活，激活率直接追踪任务难度。

Conclusion: 监督门控信号朝向正确性（而非依赖无监督置信度），使模型能够在真正需要的地方分配解剖推理资源，在保持甚至提升诊断性能的同时大幅降低计算开销，且激活率与任务难度高度一致，验证了按需计算分配的有效性。

Abstract: Deep learning models for medical image analysis typically apply a fixed amount of computation to every input, regardless of case difficulty. Anatomy-guided dual-stream architectures have been shown to improve diagnostic performance, but they evaluate both streams unconditionally, even on cases a single stream could already resolve confidently. We propose SecondOpinion, a framework in which a fast primary stream processes every case, while a second, anatomy-guided stream is invoked only when GateKeeper, a gating mechanism trained explicitly as a binary correctness classifier, judges that the primary stream's prediction needs additional scrutiny, much as a clinician might seek a second opinion on a difficult case. When activated, the two streams are combined through a lightweight cross-attention fusion module. We evaluate SecondOpinion on a unified five-class chest X-ray dataset and a pelvic fracture dataset, the latter including a held-out, harder subset of fractures that are invisible on X-ray but confirmed via CT. SecondOpinion matches or exceeds prior state-of-the-art performance on both tasks, while activating its anatomy-guided stream on only 9.23% of chest X-ray cases, rising to 24.12% on visible fractures and 45.71% on invisible fractures, an activation rate that tracks task difficulty directly. These results suggest that supervising a gating signal toward correctness, rather than relying on unsupervised confidence, allows a model to allocate anatomical reasoning where it is actually needed.

</details>


### [189] [DAVET: Denoising-Aware Visual Evidence Trajectory Allocation for Diffusion Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01821)
*Yongkang Zhou,Xiang Xia,Cheng Yan,Fan Xu,Wuyang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DAVET，一个无需训练的框架，通过在不同去噪步骤间自适应分配视觉证据资源来加速扩散视觉语言模型（dVLMs）的推理。在LLaDA-V和LaViDa两个模型上，DAVET实现了平均1.55倍的加速，性能仅下降1.86%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散视觉语言模型（dVLMs）在每一步去噪时都重复进行视觉条件计算，导致视觉条件处理成为主要的推理开销。与自回归解码不同，扩散生成会反复回顾整个响应，且视觉证据的需求随去噪阶段显著变化。现有加速方法（解码端策略或视觉token压缩）未将视觉证据视为一种随扩散过程动态变化的资源。

Method: DAVET是一个无需训练（training-free）的框架，包含三个关键组件：（1）相位条件证据轨迹（phase-conditioned evidence trajectory）作为初始分配策略；（2）通过操作需求（operation demand）设置证据储备（evidence reserve），并使用轨迹风险（trajectory risk）在每一步调节证据分配；（3）从单一视觉编码构建层级化证据视图（hierarchy of evidence views），分离"何时需要多少证据"与"证据视图如何构建"两个问题。

Result: 在两个代表性dVLMs（LLaDA-V和LaViDa）的多个视觉理解基准上评估，DAVET实现平均1.55倍加速，平均相对性能下降仅1.86%。

Conclusion: 去噪感知的视觉证据分配策略能够在大幅降低视觉条件计算成本的同时，基本保持生成质量，证明了在扩散过程中按需分配视觉证据的有效性。

Abstract: Diffusion vision-language models (dVLMs) iteratively denoise masked responses while conditioning each denoising step on visual evidence, making visual conditioning a substantial recurring inference cost. Unlike autoregressive decoding, diffusion generation repeatedly revisits the entire response as uncertainty evolves. Our analysis reveals that visual evidence demand is strongly step-dependent, motivating adaptive allocation across denoising steps. Existing inference acceleration methods operate through decoding-side strategies or visual token compression via pruning and merging, but do not explicitly treat visual evidence as a resource whose demand evolves across the diffusion process. Therefore, we present Denoising-Aware Visual Evidence Trajectory Allocation (DAVET), a training-free framework that allocates visual evidence according to the evolving generation state. Starting from a phase-conditioned evidence trajectory, the proposed allocation policy uses operation demand to set an evidence reserve whose allocation at each denoising step is modulated by trajectory risk. DAVET realizes the resulting budgets through a hierarchy of evidence views constructed from a single visual encoding, separating when and how much evidence is needed from how the evidence views are constructed. Evaluated on two representative dVLMs, LLaDA-V and LaViDa, across multiple visual-understanding benchmarks, DAVET achieves an average speedup of 1.55$\times$ with an average relative performance drop of 1.86\%, showing that denoising-aware visual evidence allocation can reduce visual conditioning cost while largely preserving generation quality.

</details>


### [190] [Detail Continuation over a Trustworthy Coarse Scale for Autoregressive Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01823)
*Hongyi Fang,Jiahui Wu,Yichen Yue,Benjia Zhou,Dan Zeng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: K2N提出将VAR-based超分辨率的生成路径从全路径1-to-N自回归重构为k-to-N细节延续过程，即直接从低分辨率输入建立早期粗尺度状态，仅对剩余细尺度进行自回归恢复，以缓解生成超分辨率中的幻觉问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式超分辨率(GSR)中幻觉问题依然存在，重构结果可能包含视觉上合理但缺乏低分辨率输入支持的内容。现有VAR-based SR方法仍沿用原始完整的1-to-N自回归生成路径，但超分辨率中粗尺度信息通常更可靠，而长自回归链会累积预测误差。因此需要显式地在尺度层面重新设计生成路径。

Method: K2N将VAR-based超分辨率从全路径生成重构为k-to-N细节延续过程：早期粗尺度状态直接从低分辨率输入建立（跳过自回归预测），仅对剩余的较细尺度进行自回归恢复，从而减少长自回归链带来的误差累积和幻觉生成。

Result: K2N在标准SR指标上与VARSR基线保持竞争力，在幻觉聚焦评估（hallucination-focused evaluation）上展现出更明显的优势。

Conclusion: 显式地在尺度层面重新思考生成路径是提升生成式超分辨率可靠性的有前景方向。粗尺度信息的直接建立可减少自回归误差累积，为生成式SR的可靠性改进提供新思路。

Abstract: Hallucination remains a persistent challenge in generative super-resolution (GSR), where reconstructed results may contain visually plausible yet weakly supported content, structural deviations, or unnatural textures with respect to the low-resolution (LR) input. Existing GSR methods have extensively explored the trade-off between perceptual realism and reconstruction fidelity, but the division between preserving reliable coarse-scale information and restoring more uncertain fine details is often handled implicitly within the overall restoration process. Visual autoregressive (VAR) modeling provides a natural opportunity to revisit this issue, as its coarse-to-fine next-scale prediction offers an explicit scale-wise generation interface. However, existing VAR-based SR methods still inherit the original full 1-to-$N$ autoregressive generation path, even though, for super-resolution, coarse-scale information in LR is often relatively more reliable, while long autoregressive chains may accumulate prediction errors. Motivated by these observations, we propose \textbf{K2N}, which reformulates VAR-based SR from full-path generation into a $k$-to-$N$ detail continuation process. Specifically, early coarse-scale states are established directly from LR, while only the remaining finer scales are restored autoregressively.
