We introduce GEOID-Flood, a large-scale multi-modal flood segmentation benchmark, derived from Copernicus Emergency Management Service activations, spanning 219 events across 65 countries over ten years. The dataset provides more than 14,000 tiles with co-registered pre- and post-event Sentinel-1, in GRD and RTC format, pre-event Sentinel-2 composite, and DEM, including manually validated labels that separate background from permanent water and flooded water. Using this benchmark, we evaluate foundation models against conventional encoders across single-image, multi-temporal, and multi-modal protocols. We report three main findings: foundation models offer a consistent but modest advantage; optical-SAR fusion with finetuning best resolves transient flooding; and models trained on GEOID-Flood transfer to unseen events better than those trained on existing datasets. Dataset and code available at https://github.com/links-ads/geoid-flood.

</details>


### [256] [Global-Scale Self-Supervised Spatiotemporal Learning for NDVI Time-Series Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02322)
*Ang Li,Menghui Jiang,Xiaobin Guan,Dong Chu,Huanfeng Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GloSSR，一个全球尺度的自监督时空框架，用于NDVI时间序列重建。该框架通过人为退化相对干净的NDVI观测数据构建自监督训练对，并采用双向Transformer与ConvLSTM结合的端到端时空学习网络，配合时间通道注意力和时空先验约束，在MODIS和AVHRR数据上均取得优于对比方法的重建效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云污染和噪声导致NDVI时间序列不完整，现有深度学习重建方法受限于难以获取同一时空位置上成对的清晰与退化NDVI数据，亟需一种无需配对标签的自监督重建方法。

Method: 1) 利用真实的云污染模式人为退化相对干净的NDVI观测，构造自监督训练对，模拟真实退化过程；2) 设计双向Transformer联合ConvLSTM的端到端时空学习网络，同时捕获长距离时间依赖与短期时空相关；3) 引入基于时间通道注意力的重建模块增强信息特征；4) 设计时空先验约束，在优化过程中保持细尺度结构与长期物候趋势。

Result: 在MODIS NDVI数据上的人工退化像元重建实验中，GloSSR一致优于对比方法；基于真实观测的时间序列分析表明其能准确刻画植被动态并捕获关键物候状态；长期植被趋势分析和AVHRR数据迁移性分析验证了框架的可扩展性和广泛适用性。

Conclusion: GloSSR通过自监督学习策略解决了配对数据稀缺问题，实现了对云污染和噪声干扰NDVI时间序列的准确高效重建，并具备良好的跨传感器可迁移性，可支撑大规模环境监测应用。

Abstract: Accurate and efficient reconstruction of cloud-contaminated and noise-corrupted NDVI time series remains a challenge in remote sensing. Deep learning provides a promising solution for modeling complex spatiotemporal dependencies; however, its application is often limited by the difficulty of obtaining paired clear-sky and degraded NDVI data for identical spatiotemporal locations. To address this issue, we propose GloSSR, a Global-scale Self-supervised Spatiotemporal framework for NDVI Reconstruction. The framework constructs supervisory signals by artificially degrading relatively clean NDVI observations with realistic cloud contamination patterns, producing self-supervised training pairs that closely mimic real-world degradation. It further introduces an end-to-end spatiotemporal learning network that jointly captures long-range temporal dependencies and short-term spatiotemporal correlation through a bidirectional Transformer with a ConvLSTM architecture. A temporal-channel attention-based reconstruction module is incorporated to enhance informative features, while a spatiotemporal prior constraint is designed to preserve both fine-scale structures and long-term phenological trends during optimization. Extensive evaluations on MODIS NDVI data demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework across both artificial and real-world scenarios. In artificial degraded-pixel reconstruction experiments, GloSSR consistently outperforms the comparison methods. Time-series analyses based on real observations further demonstrate that the proposed framework can accurately characterize vegetation dynamics and capture the key phenological states. Long-term vegetation trend analysis and the transferability analysis to AVHRR data validate the scalability of the framework and illustrate its broad applicability for large-scale environmental monitoring.

</details>


### [257] [Implicit Neural Representations for Multimodal Longitudinal Image Imputation and Interpolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02324)
*Sina Wendrich,Lukas Förner,Zoe Reinke,Kartikay Tehlan,Ansgar Berlis,Michael Frühwald,Matthias Wagner,Thomas Wendler*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种患者特定的条件隐式神经表示（INR）框架，将多模态纵向MRI建模为世界坐标、时间和模态条件的连续函数，通过随机模态dropout处理缺失数据，并利用自一致性置信度估计器在推理时评估重建质量。在儿童脑肿瘤患者的纵向MRI数据上验证了其对T1CE和FLAIR的统计显著改进（p<0.05），MS-SSIM达0.95±0.02。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实临床纵向多参数MRI数据存在序列缺失、采集协议异质和跨时间点空间分辨率不一致等问题，现有方法难以在固定体素网格上进行有效的时空插值，需要一种能够处理不完全数据和异质协议的统一建模方案。

Method: 提出患者特定的条件隐式神经表示（INR），将多模态纵向MRI建模为世界坐标、时间与模态条件的连续函数；采用随机模态dropout训练策略处理不完整输入；利用连续坐标空间公式实现无需重采样到固定体素网格的时空插值；基于跨模态重建性能推导出自一致性置信度估计器。

Result: 在儿童脑肿瘤患者的纵向MRI数据上，该方法在线性插值对比中于T1CE和FLAIR上取得了统计显著改进（p<0.05），T1CE的平均MS-SSIM为0.95±0.02；预测置信度与真实重建质量强相关（Pearson r最高达0.996）。

Conclusion: 该框架在异质临床环境中展现出可靠的部署潜力，能够有效处理临床纵向MRI数据中的不完整与不一致问题，兼具精确重建和质量评估能力。

Abstract: Longitudinal multiparametric MRI is central to follow-up imaging in oncology, yet real-world clinical data are characterised by missing sequences, heterogeneous acquisition protocols, and varying spatial resolutions across time points. We propose a patient-specific conditional implicit neural representation (INR) that models multimodal longitudinal MRI as a continuous function of world coordinates, time, and modality conditioning. The model is trained with stochastic modality dropout to handle incomplete data, and its continuous coordinate-space formulation enables both spatial and temporal interpolation without resampling to a fixed voxel grid. A self-consistency-based confidence estimator is derived from cross-modal reconstruction performance at inference time. We evaluate the framework on longitudinal MRI from paediatric brain tumour patients, demonstrating statistically significant improvements over linear interpolation for T1CE and FLAIR (p < 0.05), with mean MS-SSIM of 0.95 $\pm$ 0.02 for T1CE. Predicted confidence correlates strongly with true reconstruction quality (Pearson r up to 0.996), suggesting reliable deployment potential in heterogeneous clinical settings.

</details>


### [258] [Context-Aware Mixture of Domain Experts for Bodily Expression of Emotion in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02331)
*Mohammad Mahdi Dehshibi,David Masip*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出上下文感知的混合领域专家模型（CA-MoDE），通过将场景和物体信息作为情感合理性的结构化先验分布来调节身体专家的预测，并采用最大背书门控策略融合多领域信号，在Body Language Database上取得0.3269的情感识别分数，仅用单张静态图像即超越现有使用时间模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相同的身体姿态在不同上下文中可能表达完全不同的情感，但现有方法仅将场景和物体线索作为辅助特征增强，而非作为情感合理性的结构化先验，导致上下文信息未能充分发挥作用。

Method: CA-MoDE包含三个部分：(1) 专门的场景专家和物体专家，分别生成基于各自领域条件的情感类别的软分布；(2) 这些领域条件软预测作为结构化上下文先验，在分布层级而非特征层级调制身体专家的预测；(3) 提出任务定制的最大背书门控策略，在情感维度上选择跨专家最强的上下文信号，避免冲突或无信息上下文分布平均化造成信号稀释。

Result: CA-MoDE在Body Language Database上达到0.3269的情感识别分数，使用单张静态图像即在性能上超越现有使用时间模型的方案，证明显式建模结构化空间上下文可作为行为动态的互补判别代理。

Conclusion: 显式建模结构化空间上下文（场景和物体）能够作为视频中行为动态的互补判别代理，在静态图像上实现超越时间模型的体态情感识别效果，表明分布级上下文先验建模优于特征级特征增强。

Abstract: The same body posture can convey entirely different emotions depending on its surrounding context, yet most methods for recognising bodily emotions treat scene and object cues as auxiliary feature augmentations rather than as structured priors over the plausibility of emotions. We introduce the Context-Aware Mixture of Domain Experts (CA-MoDE) for bodily emotion recognition. CA-MoDE incorporates dedicated scene and object experts to generate soft distributions over emotion categories conditioned on their respective domains. These domain-conditioned soft predictions serve as structured contextual priors that modulate the body expert's predictions at the distributional level rather than at the feature level. To fuse these multi-domain signals, we propose a task-tailored max-endorsement gating strategy that selects the strongest contextual signal across experts for each emotion dimension. Our gating strategy mitigates the signal dilution that typically occurs when conflicting or uninformative context distributions are averaged. CA-MoDE achieves an Emotion Recognition Score of 0.3269 on the Body Language Database. By outperforming existing temporal models using only single still images, our framework demonstrates that explicitly modelling structured spatial context can serve as a complementary discriminative proxy for the behavioural dynamics typically captured by video.

</details>


### [259] [Loop-Mamba: A Loop Mamba with Degradation-Aware and Shared Memory for Old Photo Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02346)
*Runci Bai,Yucheng Xin,Pu Wang,Yongcong Wang,Chen Wu,Dianjie Lu,Guijuan Zhang,Pengwen Dai,Guangwei Gao,Siyuan Yao,Zhuoran Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Loop-Mamba是一种轻量级基于循环状态空间的旧照片修复框架，通过持续传播和迭代精化的渐进状态演化来处理多种耦合退化（划痕、裂纹、褪色、模糊、噪声和缺失区域），在SynOld基准上优于现有最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旧照片往往同时遭受多种相互耦合的退化（如划痕、裂纹、褪色、模糊、噪声和缺失区域），同时损害视觉质量和语义内容。现有基于CNN和Transformer的迭代修复框架存在计算开销大和梯度稀释的问题，因此需要一个更高效且能处理异构退化并保持长距离结构重建的解决方案。

Method: 提出Loop-Mamba轻量级循环状态空间框架。核心组件包括：(1) Semantic-Guided Degradation Estimator (SGDE)，联合预测局部退化图和全局退化分数以显式建模异构退化；(2) Shared Structural Memory Mamba (S²M-Mamba)，在迭代间维护持久恢复状态，通过共享结构记忆实现鲁棒的长距离结构重建；(3) 利用一阶状态递归，通过循环状态转换而非重复堆叠深层特征变换来传播潜在恢复状态，缓解梯度稀释并避免迭代CNN/Transformer框架的计算开销；(4) 轻量级多方向扫描策略增强方向信息聚合和结构连续性；(5) 提出任务导向的Old Photo Damage Recovery Score (ODRS)联合评估退化恢复和结构重建保真度。

Result: 在公开的SynOld基准上，Loop-Mamba在传统恢复指标和新提出的ODRS指标上均一致优于现有的最先进方法。

Conclusion: Loop-Mamba通过状态空间模型固有的循环递归优势，有效解决了旧照片修复中异构退化建模和长距离结构重建的挑战，实现了更高效、更鲁棒的修复性能，同时避免了迭代深度学习框架的计算瓶颈。

Abstract: Old photographs often suffer from multiple coupled degradations, including scratches, cracks, fading, blur, noise, and missing regions, severely degrading both visual quality and semantic content. We propose Loop-Mamba, a lightweight loop-based state-space framework that formulates old photo restoration as progressive state evolution, where a persis- tent restoration state is continuously propagated and refined through iterative computation. Specifically, we introduce a Semantic-Guided Degradation Estimator (SGDE) to explicitly model heterogeneous degradations by jointly predicting local degradation maps and global degradation scores, providing degradation-aware guidance for state evolution. We further develop a Shared Structural Memory Mamba (S$^2$M- Mamba), which maintains a persistent restoration state across iterations, enabling persistent state evolution through shared structural memory for robust long-range structural reconstruction. Benefiting from first-order state recursion, Loop-Mamba propagates latent restoration states through recurrent tran- sitions instead of repeatedly stacking deep feature transformations, thereby alleviating gradient dilution while avoiding the computational overhead inherent in iterative CNN- and Transformer-based restoration frameworks. A lightweight multi-directional scanning strategy further enhances direc- tional information aggregation and preserves structural continuity. To better evaluate restoration quality, we introduce the task-oriented Old Photo Damage Recovery Score (ODRS), which jointly measures degradation recovery and structural reconstruction fidelity. Experimental results on the public SynOld benchmark demonstrate that Loop-Mamba consistently outperforms previous state-of-the-art methods across both conventional restoration metrics and the proposed ODRS.

</details>


### [260] [GROVE: Growing and Reasoning over Temporally Stratified Memory from Streaming Video Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02392)
*Sitong Gong,Caixin Kang,Tianyu Yan,Guo Chen,Bo Zheng,Kaipeng Zhang,Yunzhi Zhuge,Xiang Ruan,Huchuan Lu,Yifei Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GROVE是一个无需训练的框架，通过在连续视频流中因果式构建分层记忆（细粒度感知证据、时间戳时刻、连贯事件、跨日重复模式），统一支持反应式问答和主动式辅助两种行为，在MM-lifelong和EgoServe等基准上取得最优结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴助手既需要回答关于其视觉历史的问题，也需要识别历史何时对当前情境有用。现有视频记忆系统主要支持问题条件式回忆，而主动式助手通常使用独立的记忆和控制机制，缺乏统一框架。

Method: GROVE采用训练无关框架，从连续视频流中因果式生成单个分层记忆：保留细粒度感知证据，并逐步整合为带时间戳的时刻、连贯情节和跨日重复模式。每个层配对尺度原生的检索技能（定位观察、回放活动、遍历长期规律），反应式QA和主动式辅助共享该记忆与访问接口，区别仅在于检索由用户查询还是当前情境触发。

Result: 在包括MM-lifelong和EgoServe在内的多个基准上，GROVE在对比方法中取得了最佳结果。受控消融实验表明各时间层及其访问技能互补，其中模式层在证据跨越多天时提供最大收益。

Conclusion: GROVE证明了单一的分层记忆架构可以同时支持反应式和主动式两种行为，无需训练即可实现，且各时间尺度上的记忆层具有互补性，为可穿戴视频助手提供了统一记忆方案。

Abstract: A wearable assistant should both answer questions about its visual history and recognize when that history is useful to the present situation. Existing video-memory systems primarily support question-conditioned recall, whereas proactive assistants typically use separate memory and control mechanisms. We introduce GROVE, a training-free framework that supports both behaviors with one memory grown causally from a continuous video stream. GROVE retains fine-grained perceptual evidence and incrementally consolidates it into time-stamped moments, coherent episodes, and recurring cross-day patterns. Each stratum is paired with a scale-native retrieval skill for locating an observation, replaying an activity, or traversing long-range regularities. Reactive QA and proactive assistance share this memory and access interface, differing in whether retrieval is initiated by a user query or the current situation. Across multiple benchmarks including the challenging MM-lifelong and EgoServe, GROVE achieves the best results among the compared methods. Controlled ablations show that the temporal strata and their access skills are complementary, with patterns providing the largest benefit when evidence spans multiple days. Code will be available at https://github.com/SitongGong/GROVE.

</details>


### [261] [Does Explainability Transfer? A Controlled Benchmark of Attribution Methods on Vision Transformers and CNNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02396)
*Sathiyamohan Nishankar,Nethmi Pathirana,Pubudu Sanjeewani,Asanka Perera,Selvarajah Thuseethan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个受控基准测试，系统评估了13种XAI归因方法在8种代表性骨干网络（涵盖CNN、各类型ViT及混合架构）上的表现，从忠实性、定位、鲁棒性、复杂性和计算成本五个维度进行标准化评估。研究发现归因性能强烈依赖于架构，CNN上建立的排名不能可靠迁移到基于Transformer的模型；CAM类方法在常规边界框定位指标下得分高，但密集掩码评估显示这更多反映指标饱和而非精确定位；注意力rollout在全局注意力Transformer上提供稳定的解释但定位差。研究挑战了现有归因性能结论，呼吁架构感知的多维度评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有XAI归因方法有效性的证据大多建立在CNN上，缺乏对当前主流的多样化ViT架构的验证。作者旨在检验CNN时代得出的归因结论能否推广到ViT架构，填补这一研究空白。

Method: 构建标准化评估框架，从忠实性、定位、鲁棒性、复杂性和计算成本五个维度，系统评估4个算法家族中的13种归因方法，在覆盖CNN、各向同性ViT、层次Transformer、混合架构和线性注意力Transformer的8种骨干网络上进行受控比较。

Result: 归因性能强烈依赖架构，CNN上的排名不能可靠迁移到Transformer；CAM类方法在CNN和多数ViT上于边界框定位指标得分最高，但对线性注意力架构表现不佳；密集掩码评估显示这些优势主要来自指标饱和；CAM类方法在全局注意力Transformer上鲁棒性有限，注意力rollout提供稳定解释但定位差；忠实性相关性在方法间区分度有限。

Conclusion: 现有关于归因性能的结论具有架构局限性，不能简单推广到ViT。需要采用架构感知的多维度评估框架来全面衡量归因方法，忽视架构差异和指标饱和问题可能导致误导性结论。

Abstract: Most evidence on the effectiveness of explainable artificial intelligence (XAI) attribution methods has been established on convolutional neural networks (CNNs), with limited investigation into whether these conclusions generalize to the diverse Vision Transformer (ViT) architectures that now dominate computer vision. This paper presents a controlled benchmark that evaluates attribution quality across five dimensions: faithfulness, localization, robustness, complexity, and computational cost. A standardized framework assesses 13 attribution methods from four algorithmic families on eight representative backbones spanning CNNs, isotropic ViTs, hierarchical transformers, hybrid architectures, and linear-attention transformers. The results show that attribution performance is strongly architecture-dependent and that rankings established on CNNs do not reliably transfer to transformer-based models. CAM-based methods achieve the highest scores under the conventional bounding-box localization metric on CNNs and most ViTs but perform poorly on linear-attention architectures. Pixel-level dense-mask evaluation further reveals that these gains largely reflect metric saturation rather than accurate localization. CAM-based methods also exhibit limited robustness on global-attention transformers, whereas attention rollout provides consistently stable explanations with poor localization. Furthermore, faithfulness correlation offers limited discrimination between attribution methods, highlighting the limitations of single-metric evaluation. These findings challenge prevailing conclusions on attribution performance and demonstrate the need for architecture-aware, multi-dimensional evaluation. The open-source code for the evaluation framework and benchmark results is available at https://github.com/Nishan-Charlie/VIT_XAI_Bench.

</details>


### [262] [USP-Mamba: Unmixing-Derived Spectral and Structural Prompting for Hyperspectral Image Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02401)
*Shi Chen,Jie Zhang,Yicong Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出USP-Mamba框架，用于高光谱图像超分辨率重建，通过解混衍生的光谱先验和图像依赖的结构提示（空间+频率）来引导Mamba状态演化，并采用互补的Hilbert和语义引导邻域扫描策略，在多个数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Mamba-based高光谱超分辨率模型存在两个核心问题：(1) 因果序列建模要求将2D高光谱特征按预定义扫描顺序展开，破坏了空间邻接性，限制了上下文信息的有效传播；(2) 现有模型的状态空间参数化主要来源于通用学习表示，未与高光谱图像的内在特性（如材料组成）进行显式对齐。这些问题限制了对高光谱图像中长程依赖和局部细节的建模能力。

Method: USP-Mamba框架包含以下关键组件：(1) 解混衍生的光谱提示（unmixing-informed spectral prompt）：捕获输入图像的全局材料组成，提供贯穿重建过程的持久条件约束，注入Mamba序列并逐层渐进适应，引导状态演化趋向组成一致性的重建；(2) 特征级结构提示（feature-level structural prompts）：包含空间和频率两个组件，空间提示促进结构敏感的状态编码以保留局部细节，频率提示实现均匀区域与高频细节之间的区域自适应转换；(3) 互补的Hilbert和语义引导邻域扫描（Semantic-Guided Neighboring scans）：保持空间连续性并增强非局部语义依赖建模。

Result: 在多个不同数据集上的大量实验表明，USP-Mamba在定量指标和定性视觉效果上均一致优于代表性对比方法，验证了光谱先验引导状态演化、结构提示增强局部建模以及互补扫描策略的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过将高光谱图像固有的材料组成先验与图像依赖的结构提示注入Mamba状态演化过程，并配合互补扫描策略，USP-Mamba有效克服了传统Mamba模型在HSI超分辨率中空间邻接性破坏与先验不对齐的问题，展示了将领域知识融入状态空间模型的优越性。

Abstract: Hyperspectral image super-resolution aims to reconstruct high-resolution imagery while preserving dense spectral information. Recently, Mamba-based models have shown promising potential for this task by capturing long-range dependencies with linear computational complexity. Nevertheless, their causal sequence modeling requires two-dimensional hyperspectral features to be unfolded along predefined scanning orders, which disrupts spatial adjacency and restricts the effective propagation of contextual information. Moreover, state-space parameterization of existing models is predominantly derived from generic learned representations, without explicit alignment with the intrinsic characteristics of the hyperspectral image. To address this issue, we propose an Unmixing-derived Spectral and Structural Prompting Mamba framework, termed USP-Mamba, which adapts Mamba state evolution through composition-aware spectral priors and image-dependent structural prompts. Specifically, an unmixing-informed spectral prompt captures the global material composition of the input image and provides persistent conditioning throughout reconstruction. Injected into the Mamba sequence and progressively adapted across layers, it steers state evolution toward composition-consistent reconstruction. We introduce feature-level structural prompts comprising spatial and frequency components to provide image-dependent local guidance. The spatial prompt promotes structure-sensitive state encoding for local detail preservation, while the frequency prompt enables region-adaptive transitions between homogeneous regions and high-frequency details. Finally, complementary Hilbert and Semantic-Guided Neighboring scans preserve spatial continuity and strengthen non-local semantic dependency modeling. Extensive experiments on different datasets demonstrate that the proposed method consistently outperforms representative approaches.

</details>


### [263] [Loggia dei Lanzi: AI Thermography Enhancement Comparisons through 3D Photogrammetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02404)
*Scott McAvoy,Jonathan Klingspon,George Bent,Dave Pfaff,Aviral Agarwal,Maurizio Seracini,Falko Kuester*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文报告了2025年12月在佛罗伦萨领主广场佣兵凉廊开展的热成像测绘活动，使用FLIR T1020 HD相机揭示了灰泥层下的隐藏建筑特征（如封堵开口和材料过渡），并通过比较原生分辨率、FLIR硬件超分辨率（UltraMax）和AI超分辨率三种图像分辨率在SfM摄影测量流程中的表现，评估了AI图像增强在文化遗产热成像文献记录中的应用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热成像技术在文化遗产记录中日益重要，但低分辨率热图像限制了三维热模型的精度和密度。硬件超分辨率（如FLIR UltraMax）与新兴AI超分辨率模型的相对优劣尚缺乏系统性的直接对比，本研究旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在2025年12月的有利冬季环境条件下，使用FLIR T1020 HD热像仪对佣兵凉廊进行热成像采集。在SfM摄影测量流程中设置三种分辨率级别的对比实验：原生分辨率、FLIR硬件像素位移超分辨率（UltraMax）、以及最先进的AI超分辨率图像模型。通过量化各分辨率级别对特征检测和连接点生成的影响，评估超分辨率（硬件或AI）是否转化为更密集、更精确的三维热模型。

Result: 研究量化了每种分辨率级别对特征检测和连接点生成的效果，评估了硬件超分辨率和AI超分辨率在热摄影测量工作流中的实际增益。所有数据集已公开，并集成到交互式三维档案框架及城市级扩展现实（XR）叠加应用中。

Conclusion: 该研究为人工智能与遗产热成像的交叉领域提供了直接贡献，通过在热摄影测量工作流中对硬件微扫描与AI超分辨率进行对比，为文化遗产热成像文献记录中图像增强技术的选择提供了实证依据。

Abstract: The Loggia dei Lanzi in the Piazza della Signoria is one of Florence's most prominent structures visited by millions every year. Its construction history spans multiple centuries of modification. This paper presents the results of a thermal imaging campaign conducted in December 2025, using a FLIR T1020 HD camera, revealing hidden architectural features including walled-up openings and material transitions beneath the plaster surface. The favorable winter ambient conditions provided a feature-rich benchmark upon which to compare the results of enhancement algorithms and artificial intelligence models. We evaluate the application of AI-based image enhancement to thermal heritage documentation through a comparison of three tiers of image resolution in a photogrammetric Structure-from-Motion (SfM) pipeline: native resolution, FLIR's hardware-based pixel-shifted super-resolution (UltraMax), and state of the art AI-upscaled imagery models. We quantify the effect of each resolution tier on feature detection and tie-point generation, assessing whether the additional detail produced by super-resolution, whether hardware or AI-derived, translates into meaningfully denser and more accurate 3D thermal models. Our results contribute to the emerging intersection of artificial intelligence and heritage thermography by providing a direct comparison of hardware microscanning and AI super-resolution within a thermal photogrammetric workflow for cultural heritage. All datasets are made publicly available and accessible within an interactive 3D archival framework, and integrated into a custom citywide extended reality overlay application.

</details>


### [264] [DF$^3$: World Modeling via Decoder-Free Feature Forecasting in Autonomous Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02428)
*Jiaming Chen,Guoan Xu,Aoshen Huang,Haozhuo Zhang,Yang Li,Wei Pan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DF³是一种无需解码器的特征预测框架，完全在潜在空间内建模世界演化，通过可学习的空间查询直接从冻结的视觉基础模型中提取未来状态表示，并直接推导任务输出，消除了解码器的计算瓶颈，在效率和灵活性上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频预测方法存在两大瓶颈：像素级生成方法过度强调与任务无关的细节，计算开销巨大；潜在空间方法虽直接预测特征，但依赖重型解码器进行状态到任务的映射，仍存在计算瓶颈。

Method: DF³将可学习的空间查询注入冻结视觉基础模型的末端模块以直接提取未来状态表示；采用轻量级统一运动感知上下文融合（MACF）机制，将粗粒度光流扭曲与细粒度潜在互相关相结合，使查询与历史令牌表示交互以显式对齐和预测下一帧特征；然后用专门的任务查询探测预测特征以完成下游任务。

Result: 在公开基准和机器人仿真器的零样本部署上，DF³取得了与最先进方法相当的性能，同时提供了更优越的效率和灵活性，适用于集成感知与控制。

Conclusion: 传统的解码器瓶颈可以被彻底消除，世界演化完全可以在潜在空间内建模并直接驱动下游任务，为集成感知与控制提供了一条高效且灵活的路径。

Abstract: Forecasting future states from video sequences is a critical challenge for autonomous robotic systems and a fundamental objective of world modeling. Prior generative methods operating at the pixel level inevitably overemphasize task-irrelevant details, leading to prohibitive computational overhead. While latent-based approaches attempt to mitigate this by predicting features directly, the persistent reliance on heavy decoders for state-to-task mapping remains a computational bottleneck. In this work, we propose Decoder-Free Feature Forecasting (DF$^3$), a novel framework that models world evolution entirely within the latent space and directly derives task outputs, completely eliminating the need for a decoder. Specifically, DF$^3$ injects learnable spatial queries into the terminal blocks of a frozen vision foundation model to extract future state representations directly. By employing a lightweight, unified Motion-Aware Context Fusion (MACF) mechanism that seamlessly integrates coarse flow warping with fine-grained latent cross-correlation, these queries interact with historical token representations to explicitly align and forecast the feature of the next frame. Subsequently, a specialized set of task queries probes these forecasted features for the downstream task. Extensive experiments on public benchmarks and zero-shot deployment in a robotic simulator demonstrate that DF$^3$ achieves performance comparable to state-of-the-art methods while offering superior efficiency and flexibility for integrated perception and control.

</details>


### [265] [Learning to Tessellate: Point Cloud Generation via Recursive Spectral Partitioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02432)
*Monan Sun,Bangzhen Liu,Huaidong Zhang,Shengfeng He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PointRSP是一个自回归点云生成框架，通过拓扑保持的递归谱分割将点云生成重构为细分割过程，使用非平衡二叉树表示和无损几何蓝图来保持拓扑关系并捕获多尺度结构依赖，配合双流级联生成器和几何校准位置编码实现高质量生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自回归点云生成模型依赖启发式标记化策略（如空间排序或随机下采样），这些策略会破坏点云的内在拓扑结构，削弱生成形状的结构连贯性。作者希望提出一种拓扑保持的标记化方法，以保留点云的拓扑关系并增强生成质量。

Method: 1) 提出拓扑感知分区自编码器，通过混合递归谱分割策略将非结构化点云分解为非平衡二叉树；2) 提出双流级联生成器，联合建模结构演化与特征合成；3) 设计几何校准的位置编码机制，利用多尺度结构中心锚定潜在嵌入，稳定级联生成的早期结构形成阶段。

Result: 广泛的实验表明，PointRSP在生成质量和多样性方面达到了最先进的性能，并展现出对复杂3D拓扑结构的强大泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过将点云生成问题重构为拓扑保持的细分割过程，并结合递归谱分割的层级表示与双流级联生成架构，PointRSP有效解决了启发式标记化导致的拓扑破坏问题，实现了更优的生成质量与多样性。

Abstract: Autoregressive models have emerged as an effective paradigm for point cloud generation. However, most existing approaches rely on heuristic tokenization strategies, such as spatial sorting or stochastic downsampling, which often disrupt intrinsic point cloud topology and weaken the structural coherence of the generated shapes. In this paper, we present PointRSP, an autoregressive framework that reformulates point cloud generation as a topology-preserving tessellation process via recursive spectral partitioning. Instead of constructing token sequences heuristically, we introduce a topology-aware partitioning autoencoder that decomposes an unstructured point cloud into a non-balanced binary tree through a hybrid recursive spectral partitioning strategy. This hierarchical representation provides a deterministic geometric blueprint that preserves topological relationships while capturing multiscale structural dependencies within a quantized latent space. To synthesize shapes in this space, we propose a dual-stream cascaded generator that jointly models structural evolution and feature synthesis. In addition, we design a geometry-calibrated positional encoding mechanism that anchors latent embeddings using multi-scale structural centers, which stabilizes cascaded generation during the early stages of structural formation. Extensive experiments show that PointRSP achieves state-of-the-art performance in generation quality and diversity, demonstrating strong generalization across complex 3D topologies.

</details>


### [266] [InfiniSplat: Implicit Gaussian Decoding for Large-Baseline Monocular View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02437)
*Jiawei Wang,Hao Yu,Yongzhen Hu,Xinyi Yang,Tao Ni,Xin Zhan,Junbo Chen,Xiaowei Zhou,Ruizhen Hu,Sida Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: InfiniSplat是一种前馈式单图像3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）框架，将像素对齐表示转变为表面对齐表示，通过几何引导采样放置2D支撑点并利用查询条件隐式解码器预测高斯属性，在跨数据集新视角合成任务上达到SOTA性能，并展示出从Hypersim合成室内训练到复杂开放世界场景的零样本泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单图像前馈3DGS方法受限于像素对齐表示，高斯从固定的图像网格位置预测，虽然能产生良好的近视图渲染，但与真实场景表面耦合较弱，在大视点偏移下难以保持连贯的结构。因此需要一种与场景表面更对齐的表示方式。

Method: InfiniSplat首先使用几何引导采样，根据深度诱导的局部表面结构放置2D支撑点；然后应用查询条件隐式解码器，在这些支撑点处从图像特征中预测高斯属性。通过将支撑点位置锚定在几何上、同时将高斯预测从固定像素中心解耦，使高斯布局更好地贴合场景表面，减少网格离散化导致的散乱基元。

Result: 在多个跨数据集新视角合成（NVS）评估中，InfiniSplat相比其他单图像前馈基线达到SOTA性能，并展示了从Hypersim合成室内训练数据到复杂开放世界场景的零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: InfiniSplat证明了从像素对齐到表面对齐的表示转变能有效提升单图像3DGS在跨数据集和多视点场景下的生成质量与泛化能力，为前馈式单图像3D重建提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Single-image feed-forward 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) aims to directly generate a renderable 3D scene representation from one input image, avoiding the cost of multi-view capture and per-scene optimization. However, existing methods are often constrained by a pixel-aligned representation, where Gaussians are predicted from fixed image-grid locations. Such pixel-aligned primitives can produce promising nearby-view renderings, but they remain weakly coupled to underlying scene surfaces and struggle to preserve coherent structures under large viewpoint shifts. We present InfiniSplat, a feed-forward single-image 3DGS framework that moves from a pixel-aligned representation toward a surface-aligned representation. InfiniSplat constructs this representation by first using geometry-guided sampling to place 2D supports according to depth-induced local surface structure, and then applying a query-conditioned implicit decoder to predict Gaussian attributes from the image features queried at these supports.By grounding support locations in geometry while decoupling Gaussian prediction from fixed pixel centers, InfiniSplat produces Gaussian layouts that better follow scene surfaces and reduce scattered primitives caused by grid discretization.Across multiple cross-dataset NVS evaluations, InfiniSplat achieves state-of-the-art performance compared with single-image feed-forward baselines, and demonstrates zero-shot generalization from Hypersim indoor synthetic training to complex open-world scenes.Project page: https://zju3dv.github.io/InfiniSplat.

</details>


### [267] [UAV-Based Environmental Monitoring of Rip-Current Indicators Using Wavelet-Derived Texture Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02448)
*Yonatan Ben Avraham,Baruch Binyaminov,Yehudit Aperstein*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种物理信息驱动的沿海环境监测工作流，将小波变换（DWT）导出的空间频率纹理特征与深度学习相结合，用于从UAV拍摄的RGB影像中检测裂流（rip current）的视觉指示。双流融合架构在图像级分类中达到95%以上准确率，通道替代方法在YOLOv8目标检测中达到94% mAP@50，并通过XAI验证模型关注视觉合理的波浪间隙区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 裂流是常见但危险的沿海自然危险，威胁海滩游客并给救生员和海岸管理者带来运营挑战。从标准UAV RGB影像中可靠监测裂流仍然困难，因为危险通道通常表现为破碎波、泡沫纹理或沉积物图案中的微妙间隙，且这些特征受光照、海况和环境噪声影响。

Method: 提出将离散小波变换（DWT）导出的空间频率纹理特征与深度学习集成的工作流。评估多种策略：从计算高效的通道替换到带注意力机制的双流融合。使用任务特定的CNN进行图像级存在分类，使用YOLOv8进行对象级定位，并与标准RGB基线对比。

Result: 在评估数据集条件下，集成小波纹理特征优于纯RGB模型。双流架构在分类任务中达到最强性能，准确率超过95%且召回率高；通道替换在YOLOv8目标检测中效果最佳，达到94% mAP@50定位精度。XAI分析定性证明模型关注与裂流相关的视觉合理波浪间隙区域。

Conclusion: 在评估数据集条件下，物理信息驱动的小波集成可能为基于UAV的可解释海滩安全风险缓解决策支持工具提供支持。

Abstract: Rip currents are recurrent coastal natural hazards that threaten beachgoers and create operational challenges for lifeguards and coastal managers. Reliable monitoring from standard RGB (red-green-blue) imagery acquired by unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) remains difficult because hazardous channels often appear as subtle gaps in breaking waves, foam texture, or sediment patterns, and these signatures are affected by illumination, sea state, and environmental noise. This study presents a physically informed coastal environmental monitoring workflow for detecting visually expressed rip-current indicators that integrates wavelet-derived spatial-frequency texture features with deep learning. We evaluate multiple strategies for incorporating Discrete Wavelet Transform features into convolutional architectures, from computationally efficient channel replacement to dual-stream fusion with attention mechanisms. Performance is assessed against a standard RGB baseline using a task specific convolutional neural network for image-level presence classification and a YOLOv8 model for object-level localization. Under the evaluated dataset conditions, integrating wavelet derived texture features improves performance over RGB-only models. The dual-stream architecture achieves the strongest classification performance, exceeding 95% accuracy with high recall, while channel replacement is most effective for YOLOv8 object detection, reaching 94% mAP@50 for localization. Explainable artificial intelligence analyses provide qualitative evidence that the models attend to visually plausible wave-gap regions associated with rip currents. These results suggest that under the conditions of the evaluated dataset, physically informed wavelet integration may support UAV-based decision-support tools for interpretable beach-safety risk mitigation.

</details>


### [268] [MoRAL: Sensor-Grounded BEV Reasoning for Compact VLMs toward Edge-Oriented Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02449)
*Ambarish Govindarajulu Kaliamurthi,Kaikai Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MoRAL是一个两阶段微调流水线，通过在BEV图像中编码物理信息（LiDAR距离色带、目标类别聚类形态、雷达多普勒速度楔形叠加），使仅52M参数（占模型总量的2.4%）的Cosmos-Reason2-2B模型在自动驾驶空间推理任务中，以仅四分之一于8B基线的参数量在八个问题类型中赢得七个，紧急制动召回率从10.8%提升至47.8%，输出退化率从94.1%降至20.8%，并可在8GB消费级GPU上以42 tok/s运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在资源受限的自动驾驶平台上部署视觉语言模型进行安全关键的空间推理，需要同时兼顾紧凑的模型尺寸和可靠的度量定位能力。现有VLM依赖大型3D骨干网络，难以在移动边缘平台运行。

Method: 两阶段微调流水线：(1) 第一阶段在60,000条grounding记录上微调视觉编码器，使模型学会解析物理编码的BEV表示（LiDAR距离编码为色带、目标类别编码为聚类形态、雷达多普勒速度编码为方向楔形叠加）；(2) 第二阶段在57,696条由Cosmos-Reason2-8B教师生成的思维链记录上微调全模型（52M参数），覆盖八种驾驶问题类型。该方法将空间感知外部化到输入图像中，推理时无需学习3D骨干网络。

Result: 在2,304个留出nuScenes帧上，由Gemma 4 (31B)校准并与人类评审验证评估：MoRAL在八个问题类型中赢得七个（相对零样本8B基线），尽管参数量少4倍；在需要结构化多步物理推理的问题类型上获胜幅度最大。紧急制动召回率从10.8%提升至47.8%，输出退化率从94.1%降至20.8%，完整流水线可在8GB GPU上以42 tok/s运行且无需量化。

Conclusion: MoRAL证明了将物理感知外部化到BEV输入图像中、结合教师蒸馏两阶段微调，能够在极少的可训练参数下实现紧凑、物理接地且可靠的车载边缘VLM推理，为移动边缘平台上的紧凑型VLM推理建立了可复现的基础。

Abstract: Deploying vision-language models (VLMs) for safety-critical spatial reasoning on resource-constrained autonomous driving platforms requires both compact model size and reliable metric grounding. We present MoRAL (Multimodal Reasoning for Autonomous Language Models), a two-stage fine-tuning pipeline that teaches Cosmos-Reason2-2B to first read a physics-encoded Bird's Eye View (BEV) representation and then reason over it for driving decisions. The BEV image encodes LiDAR metric distance as color bands, object class as cluster morphology, and radar Doppler velocity as directional wedge overlays, externalizing spatial perception into the input image so that no learned 3D backbone is required at inference. Stage 1 fine-tunes the vision encoder on 60,000 grounding records; zero-shot baselines produce no parseable BEV outputs, confirming the vocabulary requires explicit training. Stage 2 fine-tunes the full model (52M parameters, 2.4% of total) on 57,696 chain-of-thought records generated by Cosmos-Reason2-8B as teacher, spanning eight driving question types. On 2,304 held-out nuScenes frames evaluated by Gemma 4 (31B) calibrated against human review, MoRAL wins seven of eight question types over a zero-shot 8B baseline despite using four times fewer parameters, with the largest margins on question types requiring structured multi-step physics reasoning. Emergency braking recall improves from 10.8% to 47.8%, output degeneration falls from 94.1% to 20.8%, and the full pipeline fits a consumer 8 GB GPU at 42 tok/s without quantization. These results establish a reproducible foundation for compact, physics-grounded VLM reasoning on mobile edge platforms.

</details>


### [269] [ISRS-DETR: Detection-Guided Click Propagation for Remote Sensing Interactive Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02468)
*Thanh Duc Pham,Anh Nguyen,Duong Duc Hieu,Minh-Tan Pham*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ISRS-DETR，一种检测引导的交互式分割框架，利用遥感场景中同类物体间的强相关性，将单次点击传播到整类物体，大幅减少每张图片所需的点击次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交互式分割方法应用于遥感影像时，将同类所有实例当作单一目标，导致对某个物体的交互无法惠及其同类邻体，每张图像可能需要多达40次点击，实用性受限。

Method: ISRS-DETR采用RF-DETR解码器与交互式分割骨干网络相结合，定位共现的同类物体；引入Dynamic Top-K Click Selection策略，仅保留可靠的提议并将其转换为模拟点击，使一次用户交互能够传播至整个物体类别。

Result: 在三个标准遥感基准数据集上，ISRS-DETR取得了最先进的精度，同时显著降低了每张图片的点击次数（NoC-I）。

Conclusion: 利用遥感场景中同类物体间的强相关性，提出了检测引导的交互式分割框架，实现了从单目标交互到整类传播的范式转变，兼顾精度与效率，代码和数据将开源发布。

Abstract: Interactive segmentation reduces the prohibitive cost of pixel-level annotation by allowing users to delineate objects with a few clicks. However, applying this paradigm directly to remote sensing imagery is non-trivial: ultra-high resolutions, small object sizes, and sparse spatial distributions all degrade segmentation quality. Recent work has addressed the resolution barrier and achieved competitive results in interactive segmentation for remote sensing (ISRS). However, they treat all instances of a class within an image as a single objective target. Consequently, interactions spent on one object contribute nothing to its same-class neighbours, and satisfactory masks may demand up to 40 clicks per image, hindering the practicality of these frameworks. We observe that remote sensing scenes exhibit markedly strong inter-object correlation, meaning a single clicked object is highly informative about the rest of its category. Building on this, we propose ISRS-DETR, a detection-guided interactive segmentation framework that injects object-level evidence into both training and inference. Our ISRS-DETR employs an RF-DETR decoder with the interactive segmentation backbone to localise co-occurring same-class objects, and introduces a Dynamic Top-K Click Selection strategy that retains only reliable proposals and converts each into a simulated click, so one user interaction propagates across an entire class. Experiments on three standard remote sensing benchmarks show that ISRS-DETR achieves state-of-the-art accuracy while substantially reducing Number of Clicks per Image (NoC-I). All codes and data splits will be released for reproducibility upon acceptance.

</details>


### [270] [Calibrated Similarity and Graph Clustering for Open-Set Animal Re-Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02469)
*Mohamed ElBassat,Seifeldin Elkerdany,Mohamed ElBialy,Gamal Abouelhamd,Jana Ghoneim,Assem Elkady,Mohamed Elboraay,Nelly Semenova*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个面向动物再识别（animal re-ID）的"相似度到聚类"（similarity-to-clustering）流水线，应用于欧亚猞猁、火蝾螈、蠵龟和德州角蜥四类动物的图像。方法先通过分割分离目标个体，再对特定物种进行轻量预处理增强身份相关视觉线索，使用WildFusion结合MiewID全局描述子与两个局部匹配分支（ALIKED+LightGlue、DISK+LightGlue）估计成对相似度，并通过图聚类将查询样本聚类为新个体或将查询-数据库相似度用于匹配已知个体。最终集成了训练-free和微调后的MiewID变体（Dynamic ArcFace和SphereFace2-Focal），在公开ARI上达到0.72124、私有ARI达0.70393，显著优于WildFusion基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动物再识别面临开放集（open-set）挑战：系统不仅要能将查询图像匹配到已知个体，还要能发现未见过的个体并通过聚类正确分组。野外条件下光照、姿态、遮挡等视觉变化使得这一任务极具挑战性，现有方法在发现未知个体方面表现不足。

Method: 提出一个三段式流水线：(1) 分割阶段使用分割模型隔离目标个体；(2) 对lynx、sea turtle、salamander进行物种特定的轻量预处理以增强身份相关视觉线索，德州角蜥仅使用分割结果；(3) 相似度估计采用WildFusion框架，对MiewID全局描述子进行校准，并与ALIKED+LightGlue和DISK+LightGlue两个局部匹配分支融合。查询-查询相似度经精细化后通过图聚类算法生成身份簇，查询-数据库相似度用于将置信样本关联到已知身份。同时评估了训练-free和微调（Dynamic ArcFace、SphereFace2-Focal）的MiewID变体并集成。

Result: 最终集成的流水线在公开ARI达到0.72124（最佳公开ARI），私有ARI为0.70393；一个更简单的"预处理-校准"变体在私有ARI上达到0.71087（最佳私有ARI）。两者均显著优于WildFusion基线，表明校准的全局-局部融合结合物种感知的预处理策略在野外开放集动物再识别中有效。

Conclusion: 校准的全局-局部描述子融合（WildFusion）配合物种特定的预处理方案，能够有效应对野外复杂条件下的开放集动物再识别。物种感知的预处理策略和多种MiewID训练变体的集成是提升聚类与匹配性能的关键因素，代码已开源。

Abstract: AnimalCLEF26 addresses discovery-oriented animal re-identification, where systems must both attach query images to known individuals and discover unseen individuals by clustering them correctly. We present a similarity-to-clustering pipeline for this setting across Eurasian lynx, fire salamander, loggerhead sea turtle, and Texas horned lizard images. The method first isolates the target specimen using segmentation and then applies lightweight species-specific preprocessing for lynx, sea turtle, and salamander images to enhance identity-relevant visual cues, while Texas horned lizard images are used after segmentation only. Pairwise similarities are then estimated with WildFusion by calibrating and combining a MiewID global descriptor with two local matching branches, ALIKED + LightGlue and DISK + LightGlue. The resulting query-query similarities are refined and converted into identity clusters using graph-based clustering, while query-database similarities are used to attach confident samples to known identities. We evaluate training-free and fine-tuned MiewID variants, including Dynamic ArcFace and SphereFace2-Focal adaptations, and combine them in the final ensemble. Our selected ensemble substantially improves on the WildFusion baseline, achieving the best public ARI of 0.72124 and a private ARI of 0.70393, while a simpler preprocessing-before-calibration variant achieves the best private ARI of 0.71087. These results indicate that calibrated global-local fusion with species-aware preprocessing choices is effective for open-set wildlife re-identification under challenging field conditions and visual variation. The implementation code is available on GitHub.

</details>


### [271] [Grounding Agentic VLMs with Dedicated Segmentation for Fine-Grained Vehicle Damage Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02470)
*Vishwajeet Shivaji Hogale,Anjali Pai,Nitya Ravi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TinyDamage混合架构，通过将空间定位任务从视觉语言模型中分离出来交给专用的多任务分割模型，并置于7节点LangGraph智能体管线中以约束VLM的每一步生成，从而解决VLM在细粒度车辆损伤检测中空间定位不可靠的问题，将报告幻觉率从92%/78%降至31%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM在真实世界的视觉评估任务中空间定位能力不可靠，尤其在车辆损伤检测这种细粒度、视觉上模糊的目标场景下表现系统性失准（在反光区域产生幻觉、漏检细长划痕、定位输出空间不一致）。尽管VLM在此任务上语义分类准确率可达87.3%，但空间级定位严重缺失。

Method: 提出TinyDamage混合架构：1）将空间定位任务委托给专用的多任务分割模型，VLM仅负责语义推理和报告生成；2）在分割侧比较了不同损失函数对微小目标定位的影响，发现focal loss在小目标检测上效果崩塌，而监督对比学习目标显著提升损伤/背景可分离性；3）将分割模型集成到7节点LangGraph智能体管线中，对VLM每个生成步骤进行基于分割输出的接地约束。

Result: 分割侧发现focal loss导致微小损伤检测归零，监督对比损失显著改善分离度。在100份人工验证报告上，报告幻觉率从纯文本92%和纯图像78%降至31%。引入了DET_l逐类别检测指标用于类不平衡下微小目标接地的评估，并报告了管线延迟和可靠性特征。

Conclusion: 将空间接地从VLM中剥离并委托给专用分割模型，配合对比学习损失和智能体管线约束，可以实质性地解决VLM在微小损伤目标上的空间幻觉问题，为VLM在高风险视觉评估场景中的可靠部署提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly deployed as reasoning agents in real-world visual assessment pipelines, yet their spatial grounding remains unreliable for fine-grained, visually ambiguous targets. We study this gap in the context of automated vehicle damage assessment, where fine-grained defects such as scratches and hairline cracks occupy few pixels, produce weak gradient signal, and are easily confused with reflections and surface texture. We show that a state-of-the-art VLM (Qwen-VL) achieves strong semantic classification accuracy (87.3%) on this task but is systematically ungrounded at the spatial level: it hallucinates damage in reflective regions, misses elongated scratches entirely, and produces spatially inconsistent outputs when prompted for localization. We propose TinyDamage, a hybrid architecture that delegates spatial grounding to a dedicated multi-task segmentation model while reserving the VLM for semantic reasoning and report generation. On the segmentation side, we find that the choice of loss function has an outsized and underexplored effect on tiny-object grounding: focal loss, widely used for class imbalance, collapses tiny-damage detection to zero, while a supervised contrastive objective measurably improves damage/background separability. We integrate the segmentation model into a 7-node LangGraph agent pipeline that grounds every VLM generation step in the segmentation output, and show that this grounding reduces the report hallucination rate from 92% (text-only) and 78% (image-only) to 31% in a controlled evaluation on 100 human-verified reports. We introduce DET_l, a permissive per-category detection metric for evaluating tiny-object grounding under class imbalance, and report latency and reliability characteristics of the deployed pipeline.

</details>


### [272] [Action-grounded tissue affordance enables anticipatory auto-framing that lowers surgeon cognitive workload during laparoscopic surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02471)
*Jiayu Gu,Yiwei Wang,Jie Zhang,Guojun Cao,Keshen Lyu,Song Zhou,Yimeng Chen,Haorui Wang,Qingmin Feng,Shenchao Shi,Huan Zhao,Wenbin Chen,Caihua Xiong,Chidan Wan,Jing Samantha Pan,Xiong Cai,Han Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DiffeoAfford框架，利用微分同胚约束的组织追踪与器械轨迹分析，从已完成的手术视频中回顾性地生成组织可操作性热点标签，无需逐帧人工标注，训练实时预测模型用于腹腔镜手术的辅助自动取景系统AffordView，减轻外科医生认知负荷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算注意力模型有助于腹腔镜手术中的视觉管理，但需要密集空间标签，而手术意图高度专业且隐性，获取人工标签十分困难。

Method: 结合微分同胚约束的组织追踪与器械轨迹分析，从完整手术过程中回顾性地生成可操作性热点标签；利用这些标签训练实时预测模型，用于AffordView自动取景系统。

Result: 该框架与专家标注和术中外科医生注视方向一致，并在真实世界评估中通过主观、生理和行为指标降低了外科医生的认知负荷。

Conclusion: DiffeoAfford通过动作驱动的组织可操作性框架，在无需人工逐帧标注的情况下有效生成视觉注意力监督，成功实现腹腔镜手术的辅助自动取景，减轻外科医生认知负担。

Abstract: Computational attention models could help surgeons manage the visual demands of laparoscopy, but they require dense spatial labels that are difficult to obtain because surgical intent is highly specialized and tacit. Here, we introduce DiffeoAfford, an action-grounded tissue affordance framework that retrospectively derives visual attention supervision from completed surgical procedures. By combining diffeomorphism-constrained tissue tracking with instrument trajectory analysis, DiffeoAfford generates affordance hotspot labels without manual per-frame annotation. A real-time prediction model trained on these labels anticipates relevant surgical regions and enables AffordView, an assistive auto-framing system for laparoscopic visualization. The proposed framework aligns with expert annotations and intraoperative surgeon gaze, and reduces surgeon cognitive workload during real-world evaluations using subjective, physiological, and behavioral measures.

</details>


### [273] [EchoCache: Energy-Guided Cross-Modal Caching for Efficient Audio-Driven Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02474)
*Jiayu Chen,Xiaoyu Wu,Rongshan Gao,Maoliang Li,Zihao Zheng,Xinhao Sun,Hailong Zou,Guojie Luo,Xiang Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EchoCache是一个面向音频驱动视频生成（A2V）的能效引导跨模态缓存框架，通过利用音频时频能量作为显著性锚点指导潜在特征缓存更新，并引入动态时间步-潜在缓存机制与量化缓存管理，在保持生成质量和音视频一致性的同时显著提升推理速度，在Wan2.2-S2V上实现2.46倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的A2V生成依赖扩散模型的迭代去噪过程，推理开销大。已有缓存方法主要利用视觉特征的时间冗余，却忽视了A2V中音频驱动视觉生成这一跨模态对齐特性——音频在时间维度上具有高度非均匀的重要性分布，导致现有缓存方法在时间语义和计算存储两个层面存在错配。

Method: 论文提出EchoCache框架，核心包含两个部分：(1) 以音频时频能量作为显著性锚点，引导潜在（latent）级别的缓存更新策略，从而优先保留音频显著区域的视觉特征；(2) 引入动态时间步-潜在缓存机制与量化缓存管理，联合优化计算效率和内存占用。

Result: 在主流A2V模型上的大量实验表明，EchoCache在保持生成质量与音视频一致性的同时，持续改善了延迟-质量权衡。在EMTD基准上对Wan2.2-S2V模型，EchoCache实现了2.46倍加速且取得最佳综合性能。

Conclusion: EchoCache通过将音频能量信息融入缓存策略，有效解决了A2V缓存中的跨模态错配问题，为高效的音频驱动视频生成提供了新的思路，同时开源了代码以便复现和后续研究。

Abstract: Audio-driven video generation (A2V) has achieved promising progress in synthesizing temporally coherent and audio-visually aligned videos, yet its inference remains expensive due to the iterative denoising process of diffusion models. Existing caching methods mainly exploit temporal redundancy in visual features while overlooking the cross-modal alignment of A2V, where audio drives visual generation with highly non-uniform temporal importance. In this paper, we identify two levels of misalignment in existing A2V caching methods: temporal-semantic and computation-storage misalignment. To address them, we propose EchoCache, an energy-guided cross-modal caching framework for efficient A2V generation. EchoCache leverages audio time-frequency energy as a saliency anchor to guide latent-level cache updates and further introduces a dynamic timestep-latent caching mechanism with quantized cache management for joint efficiency and memory optimization. Extensive experiments on mainstream A2V models show that EchoCache consistently improves the latency-quality trade-off while preserving generation quality and audio-visual consistency. In particular, on Wan2.2-S2V over the EMTD benchmark, EchoCache achieves a 2.46x speedup with the best overall performance. Code is available at https://github.com/IF-LAB-PKU/EchoCache.

</details>


### [274] [Fermat Active Laplace Learning for Semi-Supervised Hyperspectral Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02483)
*Vutichart Buranasiri,James M. Murphy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出两种基于密度感知Fermat距离与泊松加权谐波标签传播的高光谱图像分类主动学习算法：FALL和A-FALL。FALL构建全量数据的Fermat距离亲和矩阵并运行PWLL，A-FALL则通过地标点采样和MDS近似以提升大规模场景的可扩展性。实验在Salinas A和Pavia数据集上验证了A-FALL的高效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高光谱图像分类中，标记样本稀缺是核心瓶颈。现有主动学习方法在亲和矩阵构建和标签传播时未充分考虑数据流形的密度结构，且大规模HSI场景下计算开销巨大。本文旨在结合密度感知的距离度量与高效的标签传播策略，提升分类精度并解决可扩展性问题。

Method: 1. 提出基于不确定性的采集函数，扩展PWLL框架。2. FALL：用Fermat距离构建全量数据的亲和矩阵，结合最小范数采集函数的对角扰动运行PWLL。3. A-FALL：通过最远点采样选取地标点，计算数据点与地标间Fermat距离，用landmark MDS构建亲和矩阵；多轮查询后用留一交叉验证选择Fermat指数p。

Result: 在Salinas A和Pavia数据集上的实验表明：FALL在标注精度上表现优异，A-FALL在大规模HSI场景下展现了良好的可扩展性，同时保持竞争力精度。

Conclusion: Fermat距离能够提供密度感知的流形估计，与PWLL标签传播结合可有效提升HSI分类的主动学习性能；landmark采样与MDS近似策略成功解决了大规模场景的计算瓶颈。

Abstract: Two active learning algorithms for hyperspectral image (HSI) classification are proposed that combine density-aware Fermat distances with Poisson-reweighted harmonic label propagation. Our methods actively query points using an uncertainty-based acquisition function, extending Poisson ReWeighted Laplace Learning (PWLL). Our first algorithm, Fermat Active Laplace Learning (FALL), builds an affinity matrix using Fermat distances between all data points. Then, PWLL is run with a diagonal perturbation using the minimum-norm acquisition function. In contrast, Approximate FALL (A-FALL) computes Fermat distances between each data point and landmark pixels selected via farthest-point sampling and constructs the affinity matrix using landmark multidimensional scaling. After several query rounds, A-FALL selects the Fermat exponent $p$ using a leave-one-out cross-validation variant. FALL and A-FALL leverage Fermat distances and subsequent harmonic label propagation to provide a density-aware estimation of the data manifold, improving labeling accuracy. Experiments on Salinas A and Pavia show the effectiveness of FALL and the scalability of A-FALL to large HSI scenes.

</details>


### [275] [DyFrDet: Towards Accurate Small Object Detection via Dynamic Frequency Suppression with Label Disambiguation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02495)
*Zihan Yang,Yang Guo,Hongxing Zhang,Dan Lu,Siyuan Yao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DyFrDet小目标检测器，通过动态频率感知特征金字塔网络抑制频域中的背景干扰（低频率冗余和高频噪声），并结合标签消歧模块显式建模目标标签的固有模糊性，在多个基准上达到最先进的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管小目标检测已有长足进步，但由于视觉线索不足，精确识别小目标仍具挑战性。以往工作主要致力于构建小目标的判别性表征，却严重忽视了频域中的广泛噪声和标签模糊性问题，这两大因素显著阻碍了准确定位。

Method: 提出DyFrDet检测器，包含两个核心组件：(1) 动态频率感知特征金字塔网络（DyFrFPN），将层级特征变换到频域表示，并引入动态频带预测器（DBP）自适应地抑制低频冗余和过度高频噪声，保留对小目标识别有判别力的成分；(2) 标签消歧模块（LDM），利用概率分布显式建模并缓解目标标签的固有模糊性，提升低分辨率小目标的定位精度。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，DyFrDet达到了最先进的性能，验证了其在各种挑战性场景下的有效性和鲁棒性。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 通过动态频域噪声抑制与标签消歧相结合，DyFrDet有效解决了小目标检测中频域干扰与标签模糊两大被忽视的问题，展现出优异的检测性能。

Abstract: Despite the remarkable progress over the past decades, accurately identifying small objects remains challenging because of their insufficient visual cues. Previous works typically attempt to construct discriminative representation of the small objects. However, the wide range frequency domain noises and label ambiguities have been greatly overlooked, which significantly hinders the accurate localization. To address these issues, we propose a novel small object detection (SOD) detector termed DyFrDet, which is able to precisely localize the small object by dynamically suppressing the background distractions in frequency domain. Specifically, we propose a Dynamic Frequency-aware Feature Pyramid Network (DyFrFPN) to adaptively suppress low-frequency redundancy and excessive high-frequency noises. The DyFrFPN transforms the hierarchical features into frequency domain representation, and introduces a Dynamic Band Predictor (DBP) to preserve the discriminative components for small object identification. Afterwards, we present a novel Label Disambiguation Module (LDM), which leverages probabilistic distributions to explicitly model and alleviate the inherent ambiguity of target labels, yielding efficient improvement in localization precision of the small objects with low-resolution. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DyFrDet achieves state-of-the-art performance across multiple benchmarks, indicating its effectiveness and robustness in various challenging scenarios. Our code is available at https://github.com/ManOfStory/DyFrDet.

</details>


### [276] [Token Radius Attention for Efficient Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02504)
*Jiayu Chen,Zhikun Jiang,Maoliang Li,Jiayi Luo,Jiawei Yang,Zihao Zheng,Hengyi Zhang,Guojie Luo,Xiang Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Token Radius Attention (TRA)，一种基于查询熵驱动的训练无关注意力稀疏化框架，通过将查询熵映射为分析性的token预算并转化为时间衰减半径，在保留9-19%注意力交互的情况下实现1.56x-2.05x加速，同时维持竞争力的生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频Diffusion Transformers (VDiTs)虽有高保真生成能力，但其稠密3D自注意力带来二次方计算开销。现有的头部级和块级稀疏方法在所有查询间共享计算预算，忽略了不同查询对注意力资源的需求差异，导致计算分配不合理。

Method: TRA框架的核心发现是：1) 保留密度在不同查询间变化但与注意力熵呈对数线性相关；2) 主导交互形成以查询为中心的邻域，其半径依赖token。方法包括：将查询熵映射为分析性token预算，并将其转化为时间衰减的半径（无需显式键排序）；同时引入融合熵提取、预热复用和分块稀疏掩码构建来进一步降低开销。

Result: 在七个Wan2.1、Wan2.2和HunyuanVideo的T2V/I2V配置上进行评估，TRA仅保留9-19%的注意力交互，实现1.56x-2.05x的加速，同时保持竞争力的生成质量。

Conclusion: TRA通过查询级自适应的稀疏注意力机制有效解决了VDiT中计算开销与生成质量之间的权衡问题，是一种无需训练且即插即用的高效注意力加速方案，代码已开源。

Abstract: Video Diffusion Transformers (VDiTs) enable high-fidelity generation but incur quadratic cost from dense 3D self-attention. Existing head- and block-level sparse methods share computation budgets across queries, overlooking token-specific attention demand. We observe that retained density varies across queries yet correlates log-linearly with attention entropy, while dominant interactions form query-centered neighborhoods with token-dependent radii. Based on these findings, we propose Token Radius Attention (TRA), a training-free framework that maps query entropy to an analytic token budget and converts it into a temporally decayed radius without explicit key ranking. Fused entropy extraction, warm-up reuse, and block-sparse mask construction further reduce overhead. Across seven Wan2.1, Wan2.2, and HunyuanVideo T2V/I2V configurations, TRA retains only 9-19% of attention interactions and achieves 1.56x-2.05x speedup with competitive generation quality. Code is available at https://github.com/IF-LAB-PKU/Token-Radius-Attention.

</details>


### [277] [ReMiX-MAE: Learning Missing-Channel Cross-Modal Representations from RGB-Only Clinical Facial Videos for Sympathetic-Mediated Pain Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02561)
*Nan Bi,Taoyue Wang,Lijun Yin,Vandana Sharma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ReMiX-MAE（可重构缺失通道的跨模态掩码自编码器），一种自监督多模态掩码预训练框架，利用同步的RGB、热成像和深度视频学习可迁移的面部表征，并显式训练对模态缺失的鲁棒性，从而支持仅RGB部署。同时收集了带视频级自我报告和纵向治疗轨迹的临床疼痛数据集SMP，实验证明ReMiX-MAE在RGB-only部署下优于RGB-only掩码自编码器基线，且在外部数据集上表现出更稳健和标签高效的迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床中的自动疼痛评估面临两大挑战：一是缺乏临床接地气的面部视频数据，只能获得序列级别的弱标签（自我报告）；二是疼痛线索在RGB图像中可能微妙或近中性，而热成像和深度信号虽信息丰富却难以在日常临床中常规部署。因此需要一个既能利用多模态信息进行预训练、又能在实际部署时仅依赖RGB的解决方案。

Method: ReMiX-MAE采用自监督多模态掩码预训练框架，从同步的RGB、热成像和深度视频中学习可迁移的面部表征，通过显式的模态缺失训练策略增强对缺失模态的鲁棒性。具体包括：收集SMP数据集（含多次就诊的前后配对记录和视频级自我报告）；在RGB-only部署下，分别通过直接特征提取和从RGB解码的伪多模态特征两种方式评估模型性能。

Result: 在SMP数据集上，ReMiX-MAE在RGB-only部署下持续优于RGB-only掩码自编码器基线，其中伪多模态特征在具有挑战性的五类设置中带来额外增益。在外部数据集上，ReMiX-MAE表现出比RGB-only基线更稳健和标签高效的迁移能力，尤其在数据有限的临床场景中优势显著。

Conclusion: ReMiX-MAE通过跨模态掩码自编码与模态缺失鲁棒性训练，成功弥合了多模态信息丰富性与临床实际仅RGB可部署性之间的鸿沟，为数据稀缺的临床疼痛评估提供了更鲁棒、标签更高效的表示学习方法。

Abstract: Automated pain assessment in real clinics is limited by scarce clinically grounded facial video data with weak labels (often sequence-level self-report) and by the fact that pain cues can be subtle or near-neutral in RGB, while thermal and depth signals are informative yet impractical to deploy routinely. To address these challenges, we propose ReMiX-MAE (Reconstructing Missing Channel Cross-Modal Masked Autoencoder), a self-supervised multimodal masked pretraining framework that learns transferable facial representations from synchronized RGB, thermal, and depth videos and explicitly trains robustness to missing modalities, enabling RGB-only deployment. To fill the gap of clinically grounded facial pain data with video-level self-report and longitudinal treatment trajectories, we collect the Sympathetic Mediated Pain (SMP) dataset with paired pre- and post-recordings across multiple visits. Under RGB-only deployment, we evaluate ReMiX-MAE using both direct feature extraction and pseudo-multimodal features decoded from RGB. ReMiX-MAE consistently outperforms an RGB-only masked autoencoder baseline on SMP, with pseudo-multimodal features providing additional gains in the challenging five-class setting. Across external datasets, ReMiX-MAE further shows more robust and label-efficient transfer than RGB-only baselines, highlighting its advantage in data-limited clinical settings.

</details>


### [278] [CAPEval: A Decoupled Caption Evaluation across Understanding and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02589)
*Zhipeng Liu,Haochen Wang,Zhaoxiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CAPEval基准，将字幕质量解耦为两个独立维度——覆盖率（Coverage）和精确率（Precision），并通过10个字幕生成器和4个模型族的受控下游实验发现：覆盖率与理解任务性能强相关，而精确率则主导生成任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有字幕质量评估将覆盖率与精确率混为一谈，用单一标量评价字幕质量，无法区分字幕涵盖的视觉信息量（覆盖率）与其描述内容在图像上的可靠性（精确率），导致评估粒度不足且难以指导不同下游任务的字幕选择与优化。

Method: 设计CAPEval（Coverage And Precision Evaluation）基准，包含人工撰写的ground-truth字幕和人工验证的原子检查清单。将字幕质量解耦为两个维度：Coverage衡量字幕覆盖ground-truth事实内容的完整程度，Precision反映字幕中所有声明的事实验证正确率。选取10个字幕生成器，在四个模型家族下进行受控下游端到端实验，仅改变字幕来源作为唯一变量。

Result: 发现一致的任务依赖型分离现象：Coverage是理解性能更强的相关性指标，而Precision是生成性能的主导预测因子。该解耦评估范式提供了更细粒度的字幕质量诊断。

Conclusion: 解耦评估范式不仅能对字幕质量进行更细粒度的诊断，还能为针对不同下游任务选择和优化字幕生成器提供可操作的指导建议。

Abstract: Captions serve as a primary supervision signal for both multimodal understanding and text-to-image generation. However, previous evaluations treat the caption quality as a single scalar objective, which conflates two distinct properties: (1) how much visual information a caption covers and (2) how reliably the image supports its stated claims. To this end, we design a decoupled caption evaluation benchmark, CAPEval (Coverage And Precision Evaluation), with human-written ground-truth captions and human-verified atomic checklist items. Specifically, CAPEval decomposes caption quality into Coverage and Precision. The former quantifies how thoroughly a caption covers ground-truth factual content, while the latter reflects the factual correctness rate of all claims expressed in the caption. We select 10 captioners and further conduct controlled downstream end-to-end experiments with them from four model families, where the caption source is the only variable. Empirically, we find a consistent task-dependent dissociation: Coverage serves as the stronger correlate for understanding performance, whereas Precision acts as the dominant predictor for generation performance. This decoupled evaluation paradigm not only delivers a more fine-grained diagnosis of caption quality, but also offers actionable guidance for selecting and optimizing captioners tailored to different downstream tasks.

</details>


### [279] [VR3D: View-Robust 3D Representation Learning for Aerial-Ground Person Re-Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02598)
*Chao Ji,Shiyu Xuan,Zechao Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VR3D是一个面向航拍-地面行人重识别的视角鲁棒3D表示学习框架，通过将2D图像特征提升到统一的3D规范空间，并采用几何-语义注意力与可靠性感知融合，解决跨平台视角变化导致的遮挡和几何形变问题。在CARGO、AG-ReID.v1和AG-ReID.v2三个基准数据集上超越了现有方法，在CARGO上Rank-1提升5.63%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航拍-地面行人重识别中，不同平台视角差异巨大，导致严重遮挡和几何形变。现有方法仅在2D图像空间学习视角不变表示，剧烈视角变化使学习到的特征仍与视角偏差耦合，无法真正实现视角无关的特征表达。

Method: 提出VR3D框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) View-Robust 3D Representation Interaction (VR3I)：从单张2D观测中提取3D先验，将2D外观特征提升到规范的3D空间；通过3D Geometry-Semantic Attention建立2D patch与对应身体部位3D voxel之间的交互，基于3D空间位置将2D语义接地到3D框架中。(2) Reliability-Aware Fusion：由于视角变化和3D重建误差导致不同样本的表示可靠性不同，该方法估计样本特定的可靠性并自适应地聚合多源表示。

Result: 在CARGO、AG-ReID.v1和AG-ReID.v2三个基准数据集上，VR3D均优于近期方法。在CARGO上Rank-1准确率提升5.63%。

Conclusion: 通过将2D特征提升至统一3D规范空间并进行可靠性感知的多源融合，VR3D有效缓解了跨视角行人重识别中视角偏差耦合的问题，验证了3D表示学习在航拍-地面行人重识别任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Aerial-ground person re-identification is a challenging task due to cross-platform viewpoint variations, which cause severe occlusion and geometric deformation. Existing methods attempt to learn view-invariant representations exclusively within the 2D image space, where drastic viewpoint variations cause the learned features to remain coupled with viewpoint bias. To address this, we propose VR3D, a View-Robust 3D Representation Learning framework that maps images into a unified 3D coordinate space to achieve view-independent feature interaction. Specifically, we introduce View-Robust 3D Representation Interaction, which leverages 3D priors extracted from single 2D observations to lift 2D appearance features into a canonical 3D space. VR3I employs 3D Geometry-Semantic Attention to establish interactions between 2D patches and 3D voxels from corresponding body parts based on their 3D spatial locations, effectively grounding 2D semantics within a 3D framework. In addition, as the reliability of these representations varies across samples due to viewpoint changes and 3D reconstruction errors, we introduce Reliability-Aware Fusion, which estimates sample-specific reliability and adaptively aggregates the multi-source representations. Extensive experiments on three benchmark datasets (CARGO, AG-ReID.v1, and AG-ReID.v2) demonstrate that VR3D outperforms recent methods. For example, it achieves a 5.63% improvement in Rank-1 on CARGO. Our code will be released.

</details>


### [280] [WorldExam: Benchmarking World Models from Apparent Appearance to Inherent Reactivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02603)
*Yuxue Yang,Shuyao Shang,Jiahe Wang,Zitong Zhou,Liang Tan,Junhan Zeng,Ruizhi Li,Junyan Li,Yu Liu,Xiao Yang,Yong Li,Jun Zhu,Hongsheng Li,Tieniu Tan,Lue Fan,Zhaoxiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出WorldExam，一个用于评估可控视频生成模型作为世界模型能力的分层诊断基准，包含视觉质量、控制依从、空间一致性和世界反应性四个层级，覆盖8个任务和1,474个案例。评估20个代表性模型后发现不同范式（相机驱动、动作驱动、语言驱动）存在明显能力分割，没有模型能同时覆盖广泛任务并保持稳定性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可控视频生成模型正被用作世界模型，但评估这些模型时，现有基准主要关注视觉质量或显式指令完成情况（如检查请求的动作和交互结果是否实现），忽略了"固有反应性"这一关键能力——即模型能否根据场景状态推断世界应如何反应，并生成输入中未明确描述但合理的后续结果。因此，需要一个新的基准来填补这一评估空白。

Method: 构建WorldExam分层诊断基准，包含四个层级：视觉质量、控制依从性、空间一致性和世界反应性。该基准包含8个专门任务和1,474个案例，支持对相机驱动、动作驱动和语言驱动三类模型范式的统一评估。其中世界反应性层级专门评估场景条件反应和目标导向行为，即超越输入中显式指定内容的能力。评估了20个代表性模型。

Result: 评估结果显示模型存在明显的能力分割：相机驱动模型在相机控制上表现出色但接口不支持动态交互；动作驱动模型对主体的控制更精确但世界通常缺乏响应；语言驱动模型在交互方面表现更好但对复杂控制的遵循不够忠实。没有任何模型能够同时具备广泛的任务覆盖和稳定的高性能。

Conclusion: 高视觉质量和显式指令的满足并不能保证模型具备固有的世界反应性。不同范式模型各有优劣，当前尚无模型能在所有评估维度上表现出色，说明可控视频生成模型距离真正健壮的世界模型仍有显著差距。

Abstract: Controllable video generation models are increasingly being developed as world models. Accordingly, evaluating them in this role extends beyond the apparent appearance of generated videos to the inherent reactivity of the worlds they depict: the ability to infer from the scene state how the world should react and to generate plausible consequences not explicitly described in the input. Yet existing benchmarks mainly assess visual quality or explicit instruction fulfillment by checking whether requested actions and interaction outcomes are realized, leaving inherent reactivity underexamined. We introduce WorldExam, a hierarchical diagnostic benchmark spanning four levels: Visual Quality, Control Adherence, Spatial Consistency, and World Reactivity. It comprises 1,474 cases across eight dedicated tasks and supports unified evaluation of camera-, action-, and language-driven model paradigms. The World Reactivity level evaluates scene-conditioned reactions and goal-directed behaviors beyond what is explicitly specified in the input. Evaluation of 20 representative models reveals a clear capability split. Camera-driven models excel at camera control, but their interfaces do not support dynamic interaction; action-driven models control subjects more precisely but often leave the world unresponsive; and language-driven models perform better on interaction but follow complex controls less faithfully. No model combines broad task coverage with consistently strong performance, showing that high visual quality and explicit instruction fulfillment do not guarantee inherent reactivity.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [281] [Cost-Effective Automated Judging of Natural-Language Mathematical Proofs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00004)
*Benjamin Grayzel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究廉价开源权重模型能否替代昂贵的SOTA模型作为数学证明评分裁判。在IMO-GradingBench上，三个廉价裁判（GPT-OSS 120B、DeepSeek-V4 Flash、Gemma-4 31B）与人类通过/失败判断的一致性在统计上与Claude Opus 4.7和Gemini 3.1 Pro相当，但成本降低可达100倍。全量实验发现"三全通过"（all-three-pass）共识规则效果最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估数学推理系统中自然语言证明的分级（grading）成本高昂，前沿LLM裁判费用昂贵。本文旨在探究廉价开源权重模型是否能在保持与前沿模型相当的可靠性的同时大幅降低成本。

Method: 在200实例验证样本中对比三个廉价裁判与两个前沿裁判；扩展到1000实例全量基准，评估不同共识规则（包括多数投票和全体一致），在四轮重复运行中测量通过一致性、精确度和运行间差异。

Result: 三个廉价裁判与人类通过/失败判断的一致性在统计上与前沿模型不可区分，成本降低一至两个数量级（最高100倍）。多数投票匹配但未超越其最强成员；全一致（all-three-pass）规则达到最高通过一致性和精确度，且四轮重复中运行间差异最小。

Conclusion: 廉价裁判可以与前沿模型竞争，成本低一至两个数量级。推荐将"三全通过"作为部署默认规则，但需注意该规则是事后（post-hoc）识别得出的，有待独立复制验证。

Abstract: Grading natural-language mathematical proofs is a recurring cost in evaluating math-reasoning systems, and frontier LLM judges are expensive. We ask whether cheap open-weight models can serve as reliable judges given a candidate proof, a ground-truth proof, and a human-grading rubric. On a 200-instance validation sample of IMO-GradingBench, three cheap judges (GPT-OSS 120B, DeepSeek-V4 Flash, Gemma-4 31B) agree with human pass/fail decisions at rates statistically indistinguishable from Claude Opus 4.7 and Gemini 3.1 Pro, at up to $100\times$ lower cost. We had expected a majority vote of the three to be the best budget option; it matched the frontier but did not improve on its strongest member. Extending to the full 1000-instance benchmark and exploring consensus rules, we found that requiring unanimous agreement (all-three-pass) reaches the highest pass-agreement and precision and, on four replicate runs, the smallest run-to-run spread. The headline finding is that cheap judges are competitive with the frontier at one to two orders of magnitude lower cost; as a deployable default we recommend all-three-pass, with the caveat that this rule was identified post-hoc and warrants independent replication.

</details>


### [282] [RubricReviewer: From Direct Critique to Objective and Comprehensive Rubric-Driven Peer Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00005)
*Shuyu Guo,Wenxiang Hu,Yuyue Zhao,Yougang Lyu,Xiaohui Yan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RubricReviewer是一个完全由rubric驱动的LLM审稿框架，将rubric生成作为显式中间步骤，并融合了无训练代理(Scout)和人类对齐训练模型(Aligner)的优势，解决了现有LLM审稿器在隐含rubric和单一监督来源上的结构性局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量学术审稿面临前所未有的投稿压力，LLM被用作审稿助手，但现有LLM审稿器存在两个结构性缺陷：(1) 直接从论文映射到评审意见，底层rubric被隐式处理，将rubric推导与判断纠缠在一起；(2) 现有范式各捕获了好评审的一半——无训练代理收集广泛证据但产生无方向性的批评，而训练模型继承了人类判别性判断的噪声与覆盖不均。

Method: RubricReviewer是一个完全rubric驱动的框架：(1) 将rubric生成作为显式中间步骤，使评审生成和最终评估都基于论文自适应的rubric；(2) 结合无训练代理(Scout)收集外部证据，与人类对齐的训练模型(Aligner)消费这些证据，融合两种监督来源的优势。

Result: 在真实世界投稿上的实验表明，RubricReviewer产生的评审比现有系统显著更全面、更具判别性，并且对对抗性prompt注入攻击表现出最强的鲁棒性。消融研究进一步确认了每个组件的必要性。

Conclusion: RubricReviewer通过显式rubric生成和双监督源融合，成功克服了现有LLM审稿系统的结构性局限，在评审质量、判别力和鲁棒性上均优于基线方法。

Abstract: Peer review at major venues is under unprecedented submission pressure, motivating the use of large language models (LLMs) as review assistants. Existing LLM-based reviewers, however, face two structural limitations. First, they map manuscripts directly to reviews, leaving the underlying rubric implicit and entangling its derivation with the judgement. Second, the prevailing paradigms each capture only half of a good review: training-free agents gather broad evidence but produce undirected critiques, while training-based reviewers inherit human discriminative judgement together with its noise and uneven coverage. We introduce RubricReviewer, a fully rubric-driven framework that addresses both limitations. It makes rubric generation an explicit intermediate step, so that both review generation and the final assessment are conditioned on paper-adaptive rubrics. It further combines a training-free agent (Scout) that gathers external evidence with a human-aligned trained model (Aligner) that consumes this evidence, fusing the strengths of both supervision sources. Experiments on real-world submissions show that RubricReviewer produces reviews that are markedly more comprehensive and more discriminative than prior systems, and exhibits the strongest robustness against adversarial prompt-injection attacks. Ablation studies further confirm the necessity of each component.

</details>


### [283] [MemoryForge: Synthesize Lifelong Memory for Human-Like LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00007)
*Bohan Tang,Yiwen Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了记忆型条件化（memory-based conditioning）范式，用自传式记忆库替代静态文本画像，使冻结的LLM能动态检索情境相关记忆来模拟人类行为。作者提出MemoryForge框架，通过上下文生成器、生活组织者和多分辨率模拟器三大组件，从简短目标人设中合成终身记忆，在角色扮演和用户模拟任务上超越传统的描述性条件化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于提示词的方法依赖静态文本画像进行描述性条件化，缺乏真实生活记忆，导致智能体行为表现过于泛化，无法展现人类特有的个性化行为。

Method: MemoryForge包含三个核心组件：上下文生成器负责社会历史背景的锚定，生活组织者确保记忆按发展脉络向目标身份一致演进，多分辨率模拟器在宏观时间摘要与高保真情景经验之间取得平衡，共同从简短目标人设合成终身记忆库。

Result: 在PersonaGym角色扮演和SimulatorArena用户模拟基准上，MemoryForge合成的记忆库使冻结LLM比强描述性条件化基线在多个指标和多种LLM骨干网络上展现出更接近人类的行为表现。

Conclusion: 记忆型条件化比描述性条件化更能赋予LLM类人的个性化行为，因受认知心理学启发，以自传式记忆为基础的条件化范式是提升智能体拟人化表现的有效路径。

Abstract: Equipping Large Language Models (LLMs) with human-like personas is crucial for agentic applications, such as role-play and user simulation. Traditional prompt-based methods rely on descriptive conditioning by injecting static textual profiles, which often makes agents show generic behaviors due to a lack of realistic life memory. To fill this gap, we introduce memory-based conditioning, a paradigm inspired by the cognitive psychology, which replaces abstract profiles with an autobiographical memory base, enabling frozen LLMs to dynamically retrieve situation-relevant memory to guide their behaviors. We formalize its enabling task as customized lifelong memory synthesis and propose MemoryForge, a novel framework to synthesize such lifelong memory from brief target personas. MemoryForge has three key components: a context generator for socio-historical grounding, a life organizer for developmental coherence toward the target identity, and a multi-resolution simulator that balances broad temporal summaries with high-fidelity episodic experiences. Experiments on PersonaGym for role-play and SimulatorArena for user-simulation, show that the synthesized memory base by MemoryForge enables frozen LLMs to exhibit more human-like behaviors than strong descriptive conditioning baselines across multiple metrics and LLM backbones.

</details>


### [284] [AgentMemBench: A Systematic Benchmark for Evaluating Long-Term Memory Management Strategies in Conversational AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00009)
*Ahmed Cherif*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出AgentMemBench，一个统一可复现的基准，在相同条件下评估五种记忆管理策略在长时对话AI中的表现。实验表明外部键值存储（EKV）在所有质量维度上均占优，尤其远距离召回能力显著（LoCoMo上Recall@5达0.573），但代价是更高的内存占用（约5,100 vs ~300 tokens），体现精确性与效率间的明确权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时记忆是对话式AI的关键瓶颈。有限的上下文窗口使得智能体无法在数千轮对话中保持连贯回忆，迫切需要统一、可重复的基准来系统评估不同记忆管理策略，并深入理解其优劣与权衡。

Method: 构建AgentMemBench基准，在完全一致的条件下评测五种记忆策略：上下文窗口（ICW）、外部键值存储（EKV）、图式情景记忆（GEM）、压缩式摘要（CBS）和网络增强记忆（WAM）。在三个公开数据集（LoCoMo、MultiDoc2Dial、MSC）上，使用Recall@k、MRR、nDCG@k、Answer F1、LLM评判Faithfulness、Memory Footprint和Latency共491个标注问题轮次进行全面评估。生成与评判均采用Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct（4-bit）和贪心解码。同时评估MemGPT/Letta和HippoRAG两个已发表系统。

Result: EKV在所有质量维度上均占优（宏平均Recall@5为0.792，MRR 0.677，F1 0.156，Faithfulness 0.354）。在长距离召回上差距决定性：LoCoMo上ICW、WAM、GEM、CBS的Recall@5均不超过0.005，而EKV达0.573，证明密集检索是唯一能扩展到长时程的方法。CBS在检索上排名第二（0.556）。WAM在语料内召回上与ICW持平，因为外部结果不含语料内来源。EKV的召回优势伴随约5,100 token的内存代价（ICW/WAM仅~300），体现了明确的准确率-效率权衡。

Conclusion: 远距离记忆是长对话AI的核心挑战，图式记忆、摘要和窗口机制在长时程上失效，只有基于密集检索的外部存储（EKV）能有效扩展，但其高内存成本构成实际部署的权衡。作者开源全部代码、环境和结果工件以确保可复现性。

Abstract: Long-term memory remains a critical bottleneck for conversational AI agents, whose finite context windows cannot support coherent recall across thousands of turns. We present AgentMemBench, a unified, reproducible benchmark evaluating five memory management strategies under identical conditions: in-context windowing (ICW), external key-value store (EKV), graph-based episodic memory (GEM), compression-based summarisation (CBS), and web-augmented memory (WAM). All are assessed across three public datasets covering long-term multi-session dialogue (LoCoMo), task-oriented document grounding (MultiDoc2Dial), and persona-grounded multi-session chat (MSC), using Recall@k, MRR, nDCG@k, Answer F1, an LLM-judge Faithfulness score, Memory Footprint, and Latency over 491 annotated question turns. Generation and judging both use Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct (4-bit), with greedy decoding for determinism. Our results show that (1) EKV dominates on every quality axis (macro Recall@5 0.792, MRR 0.677, F1 0.156, Faithfulness 0.354); (2) long-range recall is decisive: on LoCoMo, where the gold turn lies many sessions back, ICW, WAM, GEM, and CBS retrieve almost nothing (Recall@5 <= 0.005) while EKV alone reaches 0.573, showing that recency windows, summaries, and entity graphs collapse at long horizons and only dense retrieval scales; (3) CBS is the runner-up on retrieval (0.556); (4) WAM equals ICW on in-corpus recall by construction, since external results carry no in-corpus provenance; and (5) EKV's recall advantage carries a footprint cost (~5,100 vs ~300 tokens for ICW/WAM), an explicit accuracy-efficiency trade-off. We additionally evaluate two published memory systems (MemGPT/Letta, HippoRAG) against the same harness, and release all code, environment, and result artefacts for full reproducibility.

</details>


### [285] [DLLM-TTS: Block Discrete Diffusion Language Model for Text-to-Speech Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00011)
*Wasim Madha,Nityanand Mathur,Hamees Sayed,Apoorv Singh,Sameer Khurana,Akshat Mandloi,Sudarshan Kamath*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DLLM-TTS将文本到语音合成建模为X-Codec2神经音频编解码器令牌上的条件块离散扩散，通过块内掩码扩散与块间顺序处理，实现RTF为0.15的高效并行生成，仅用0.6B参数和20K小时训练数据即在Seed-TTS-eval基准上取得具有竞争力的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TTS系统面临两难：自回归编解码器语言模型语音可懂度高但需要大规模模型/数据且逐令牌顺序解码速度慢；非自回归方法虽提升速度但牺牲语言准确性。作者旨在破解这一权衡，实现既高效又准确且数据高效的语音合成。

Method: 将TTS形式化为X-Codec2音频令牌上的条件块离散扩散。模型将序列分解为若干块，块内采用掩码扩散，块间顺序处理，从而同时学习局部声学连贯性和全局文本-语音对齐。推理时在块内并行预测令牌以加速生成。

Result: 0.6B参数模型仅用20K小时训练数据，即在Seed-TTS-eval基准上取得具有竞争力的性能；推理实时因子（RTF）达到0.15，体现了高效并行生成能力。

Conclusion: 块离散扩散语言模型为实现实用且数据高效的并行语音合成提供了新路径，在生成速度与语音质量之间取得了有效平衡。

Abstract: Current text-to-speech systems face a trade-off: autoregres- sive codec language models produce highly intelligible speech but require large-scale models and training data and decode tokens sequentially, while non-autoregressive approaches im- prove speed at the cost of linguistic accuracy. We present DLLM-TTS, a framework that formulates TTS as conditional block discrete diffusion over X-Codec2 neural audio codec to- kens. The model decomposes sequences into blocks and applies masked diffusion within each block while processing blocks se- quentially, learning both local acoustic coherence and global text-speech alignment. During inference, parallel token pre- diction within blocks enables efficient generation with a real- time factor (RTF) of 0.15. A 0.6B-parameter model trained on 20K hours achieves competitive performance on the Seed- TTS-eval benchmark, demonstrating that block discrete diffu- sion language models enable practical and data-efficient speech synthesis with parallel generation.

</details>


### [286] [Obshazard-bench: Benchmarking Multimodal Foundation Models for Real-Time Disaster Intelligence from Raw Earth Observation Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00012)
*Fengxiang Wang,Qiuyang Yu,Yueying Li,Mingshuo Chen,Chengchi Fei,Kaiyi Xu,Lixin Gu,Wangxu Wei,Junchao Gong,Lipeng Ma,Jiong Wang,Fenghua Ling,Wenlong Zhang,Xue Yang,Wenjing Yang,Ben Fei,Long Lan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Obshazard-bench，一个实时、观测驱动的多模态大语言模型（MLLM）灾害智能评测基准，直接整合高频卫星探测流、地面站观测、历史灾害记录和社会经济指标，涵盖8大灾害类别、28个子类别、60多个国家及120多个历史极端事件案例，并定义了与业务化灾害流程对齐的三阶段评估体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遥感基准依赖静态、事后和专家处理的数据（如网格化再分析产品），难以与灾害快速演变和严格时间约束的真实业务场景对齐，导致MLLM在灾害应急响应中的实际能力未被充分评估。

Method: Obshazard-bench绕过延迟的专家处理和物理反演流程，直接整合多源高频卫星探测流与地面观测、历史灾害记录和社会经济指标；覆盖8大灾害类别和28个子类别、60多个国家、120多个历史极端事件案例及数千个生命周期导向的VQA样本；定义三阶段评估体系：预测性危机预警（灾前风险检测与早期预测）、活跃演化推理（灾中追踪与终止预测）、多面影响量化（灾后幅度推断、人道负担估计和社会经济影响评估）。

Result: 在代表性通用和地球聚焦基础模型上的实验表明，现有模型在将原始多通道物理观测转化为具有时间锚定和决策相关的灾害推理方面存在显著局限性。

Conclusion: 现有多模态大语言模型在真实灾害应急场景中的决策支持能力仍严重不足，亟需发展能够从原始观测直接进行时间感知和决策导向推理的灾害智能模型。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) are increasingly used to interpret Earth observation data, yet their capability to support real-world disaster emergency response remains insufficiently evaluated. Existing remote sensing benchmarks largely rely on static, post-hoc, and expert-processed products, such as gridded reanalysis data, which are difficult to align with operational disaster scenarios where hazards evolve rapidly and decisions must be made under strict time constraints. To bridge this gap, we introduce Obshazard-bench, a real-time, observation-driven benchmark for evaluating disaster intelligence in MLLMs. Unlike image-centric or post-event benchmarks, Obshazard-bench directly integrates raw, high-frequency satellite sounding streams from diverse satellite sensors with concurrent ground-station observations, historical disaster records, and socio-economic indicators, bypassing delayed expert-processing and physical-inversion pipelines. The benchmark covers 8 major disaster categories and 28 sub-categories across more than 60 countries, incorporating over 120 historically documented extreme-event cases and thousands of lifecycle-oriented VQA samples. Moreover, Obshazard-bench further defines a three-stage evaluation taxonomy aligned with the operational disaster workflow: Predictive Crisis Anticipation for pre-disaster risk detection and early forecasting, Active Evolution Reasoning for in-situ disaster tracking and termination prediction, and Multi-faceted Impact Quantification for post-disaster magnitude deduction, humanitarian burden estimation, and socio-economic impact assessment. Experiments on representative general-purpose and Earth-focused foundation models reveal substantial limitations in transforming raw multi-channel physical observations into temporally grounded and decision-relevant disaster reasoning.

</details>


### [287] [What Transfers from Text to Vision? Capability Scaling Laws and Transfer Dynamics for VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00013)
*Ziran Li,Qiang Wang,Zhengyu Chen,Shanglin Lei,Borun Chen,Jingang Wang,Xunliang Cai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出首个跨模型家族的"能力驱动的多模态缩放定律"(CDMScaling)，利用从LLM文本基准中通过PCA提取的低维能力分数S来直接预测VLM基准精度，基于150多个VLM和34个LLM的实验验证了其外推能力和泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 选择LLM骨干网络是构建VLM时最具决定性的决策，但现有方法缺乏原则性：基于计算量的缩放定律无法跨模型家族泛化，也没有框架能在训练前直接预测VLM性能。

Method: 提出Capability-Driven Multimodal Scaling Law：从LLM文本基准通过PCA提取低维能力分数S，将VLM性能建模为S的函数，包含每个骨干的迁移率(transfer rate)和量化数据缩放效率的吸收率(absorption rate)。在严格受控的recipe下训练150多个VLM，覆盖34个LLM、7个模型家族进行拟合与验证。

Result: 实验表明该定律能准确将迁移率从8B参数外推到72B规模骨干，高保真预测完整VLM训练轨迹，并泛化到完全未见过的模型家族。同时发现若干可操作洞见：某些文本基准与多模态性能负相关（暴露基准博弈行为）；基础LLM优于指令微调版本作为VLM骨干（因吸收率更高、数据缩放衰减更小）；不同模型家族在迁移-吸收空间中占据不同位置。

Conclusion: 该框架将骨干选择从昂贵的经验性扫描转变为原则性、可量化的决策，为VLM骨干选择提供了理论指导和实用工具。

Abstract: Choosing the right large language model (LLM) backbone is the most consequential decision when building a vision-language model (VLM), yet it remains fundamentally unprincipled: compute-based scaling laws fail to generalize across model families, and no framework exists for directly predicting VLM performance before training begins. We propose the Capability-Driven Multimodal Scaling Law, the first cross-family framework that predicts VLM benchmark accuracy from directly observable textual capability. Given a low-dimensional capability score $S$ extracted from LLM textual benchmarks via PCA, we model VLM performance as a function of $S$, with a per-backbone transfer rate and an absorption rate that quantifies data-scaling efficiency. To fit and validate the framework, we train over 150 VLMs on 34 LLMs spanning 7 model families under a strictly controlled recipe.
